Motivation: 结构保持的去标识化用逼真的同类型替代词替换受保护健康信息（PHI），以保持临床文本流畅性和下游工具的可用性。但关键问题是：这种替代是否会破坏下游PHI检测器所依赖的信号？现有评估方法混淆了覆盖率和效用，且在小样本下传统的零假设显著性检验信息量不足。

Method: 提出成对多检测器评估协议，包含三个核心设计：(1) 仅在掩码跨度上评分效用，将覆盖范围与效用解耦；(2) 使用TOST等价性检验替代零假设显著性检验；(3) 构建替代失败类型学，区分可修复的生成器缺陷与检测器固有局限。实验覆盖11个检测器、7个基准数据集、7种语言（共1750份文档，57000组成对跨度）。

Result: 掩码跨度召回率从76.1%降至74.9%，等价性检验显示该变化在±2个百分点边界内统计等价于零（p≈3e-9），检测器排名保持不变。残余损失集中在畸形替代词（如"Chicago"截断为"Illino"）和显著性丧失的分布外替代词（如"Cedars-Sinai"替换为"Vidant"），而非检测器对PHI识别能力下降。

Conclusion: 结构保持的去标识化不会实质性地损害下游PHI检测器性能，观察到的微小损失源于生成器缺陷而非检测器退化。该评估协议可作为审计任何结构保持变换的通用框架，相关数据集、评分代码和交互式仪表板已开源。

Abstract: Structure-preserving de-identification replaces protected health information (PHI) with realistic same-type surrogates -- "Anna S." becomes "Maria S.", not [NAME] -- so that clinical text stays fluent and downstream tools keep working. But this only helps if the substitution does not itself corrupt the signal those tools rely on. We ask a narrow, testable question: on the spans a de-identifier actually masks, can downstream PHI detectors still find the surrogate? We introduce a paired, multi-detector evaluation protocol that (i) scores utility only on masked spans, decoupling coverage from utility; (ii) uses equivalence testing (TOST) rather than null-hypothesis significance testing, which is uninformative at our sample size (57k paired spans); and (iii) builds a surrogate-failure typology separating fixable generator defects from intrinsic detector limits. Across 11 detectors, 7 benchmarks, and 7 languages (1,750 documents), recall on masked spans moves from 76.1% to 74.9% -- a change our equivalence test shows is statistically equivalent to zero within a +/-2-point margin (p ~ 3e-9), with detector ranking preserved. The residual loss does not reflect detectors getting worse at PHI: it concentrates in malformed and out-of-distribution surrogates (truncation Chicago -> Illino, salience loss Cedars-Sinai -> Vidant). A redaction floor and an open-source surrogate baseline indicate the effect is a property of well-formed substitution, not of one tool. We release the evaluation subsets, scoring code, and an interactive dashboard at https://custodianai.pages.dev so the protocol can audit any structure-preserving transform.

</details>


### [237] [Diversity is Not Ambiguity: Toward Accurate and Efficient Ambiguity Detection for Open-Domain QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03177)
*Jiwon Lee,Yong-chan Park,Jungin Hong,U Kang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ARCHIVE 是一种基于逻辑冲突的歧义检测框架，通过级联假设检验与冲突验证，结合轻量级早退编码器和冲突推理模块，在 QuireQA 基准上以 16 倍速度优势超越现有方法，F1-amb 提升最高 10.4%，F1-unamb 提升最高 21.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放域问答中的歧义检测方法存在两个核心缺陷：(1) 将答案多样性与歧义混为一谈，导致预测不准确；(2) 对所有查询采用统一处理方式，造成计算资源浪费。作者旨在提出一种更精确、更高效的歧义检测框架来解决上述问题。

Method: 提出 ARCHIVE 框架：(1) 采用级联架构，先通过轻量级早退编码器（early-exit encoder）快速处理表面可判别的简单案例；(2) 对复杂案例进入冲突推理模块（conflict reasoning module），对候选答案之间的逻辑关系建模，核心判据为——若查询的所有有效答案无法在同一解释下同时为真，则该查询存在歧义；(3) 引入不变性目标（invariance objective）增强对噪声答案集的鲁棒性。同时构建了包含 4,703 个查询的 QuireQA 基准，覆盖事实型、非事实型和病态查询。

Result: 在 QuireQA 基准上，ARCHIVE 较竞争方法在歧义查询 F1（F1-amb）上提升最高 10.4%，在非歧义查询 F1（F1-unamb）上提升最高 21.6%，且推理速度达到最佳竞争方法的 16 倍。

Conclusion: 以逻辑冲突而非答案多样性作为歧义判据更为准确可靠；级联架构配合早退机制实现了精度与效率的双赢。ARCHIVE 为开放域问答中的歧义检测提供了新的基准方法。

Abstract: How can question answering (QA) systems determine whether a query is ambiguous? Ambiguity detection is essential in open-domain QA, as misclassification leads to answering the wrong interpretation or unnecessary clarification. However, existing methods conflate answer diversity with ambiguity, leading to inaccurate predictions. They also process queries uniformly, resulting in wasteful computation. We propose ARCHIVE (Ambiguity Recognition via Cascaded Hypothesis Inspection and Conflict Verification), an accurate and efficient framework that detects ambiguity via logical conflict: a query is ambiguous when its valid answers cannot all be true under a single interpretation. ARCHIVE combines a lightweight early-exit encoder for surface-detectable cases with a conflict reasoning module that models logical relations among answers, reinforced by an invariance objective for robustness to noisy answer sets. We present QuireQA, a 4,703-query benchmark spanning factoid, non-factoid, and ill-formed queries. Experiments show ARCHIVE outperforms competitors, improving F1-amb by up to 10.4% and F1-unamb by up to 21.6%, while operating 16$\times$ faster than the best competitor.

</details>


### [238] [TumorBoard: Evidence-Grounded Multi-Agent Decision Support for Longitudinal Neuro-Oncology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03190)
*Yantong Liu,Zheyu Zhang,Runpeng Liu,Mu Xitang,Seong-Yoon Shin,Hyun-Ae Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TumorBoard是一个多智能体决策支持系统，通过共享纵向病例状态和可审计的声明-证据账本，协调放射学、神经病理学、分子诊断、指南和治疗规划等专科智能体，并由对抗性批评者暴露矛盾、安全调控器控制推荐发布。在360例隐藏基准测试中，系统取得动作F1=0.772、证据蕴含度=0.914，显著优于最强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经肿瘤学的临床决策需要综合解读序列MRI、病理、分子标志物、治疗史、体能状态及不断演变的指南，现有单一模型或简单集成难以实现协调一致的多源信息整合与安全可控的推荐输出，因此需要一种结构化多智能体协调机制来解决这一挑战。

Method: TumorBoard系统由以下核心组件构成：(1)共享纵向病例状态，统一维护患者全病程信息；(2)可审计声明-证据账本，记录每个声明的来源与依据；(3)五个专科智能体（放射学、神经病理学、分子诊断、指南、治疗规划），各自生成带来源标注的原子声明；(4)对抗性批评者，主动暴露声明间的矛盾；(5)安全调控器，根据证据充分性和时效性决定释放、附条件或延迟推荐。消融实验对账本、批评者和调控器分别进行了验证。

Result: 在360例隐藏基准测试上（匹配token预算）：(1)动作F1=0.772，证据蕴含度=0.914；(2)较最强typed-council基线高出3.1个百分点（95%CI:1.6-4.7，调整后p=0.0012）；(3)推荐-证据覆盖率达0.927；(4)证据删除条件下，系统延迟了84.2%的不安全案例，有害推荐仅5.8%；(5)安全调控器使有害发布减少7.8个百分点，代价为错误延迟增加4.3个百分点。消融实验证实了各组件缺失时出现预期的失效模式。

Conclusion: TumorBoard通过结构化多智能体协调——即共享状态、声明-证据账本、对抗性批评和安全调控——实现了优于简单集成基线的决策性能，表明结构化协调是多智能体系统优势的关键来源，为高 stakes 医学决策支持提供了可审计、可调控的架构范式。

Abstract: Neuro-oncology decisions require coordinated interpretation of serial MRI, pathology, molecular markers, treatment history, performance status, and evolving guidelines. We present TumorBoard, a multi-agent decision-support system built around a shared longitudinal case state and an auditable claim-evidence ledger. Specialist agents for radiology, neuropathology, molecular diagnosis, guidelines, and therapy planning produce atomic claims with provenance. An adversarial critic exposes contradictions, and a safety governor releases, qualifies, or defers recommendations according to evidence sufficiency and temporal validity. On a 360-case hidden benchmark at a matched token budget, TumorBoard achieved an action F1 of 0.772 and evidence entailment of 0.914. It exceeded the strongest typed-council baseline by 3.1 percentage points (95% CI: 1.6 to 4.7, adjusted p = 0.0012), while recommendation-to-evidence coverage reached 0.927. Under evidence deletion, the system deferred 84.2% of unsafe cases and limited harmful recommendations to 5.8%. The safety governor reduced harmful release by 7.8 percentage points at a false-deferral cost of 4.3 percentage points. Ablation studies of the ledger, critic, and governor produced the predicted failure patterns, establishing structured coordination as the source of the measured multi-agent advantage.

</details>


### [239] [When Refusal Looks Safe: The Refusal-Cue Shortcut in Safety Guard Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03201)
*Yu Feng,Chunting Zang,Chen Shen,Rui Miao,Ge Teng,Weidong Cai,Jieping Ye*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 安全护栏训练数据中存在"拒绝表达-无害标签"的共现偏斜，导致模型学习到"拒绝提示捷径"——在有害回复中插入拒绝语即可翻转判定。本文通过稀疏互补掩码这一轻量级后处理干预手段，定位并抑制捷径相关的注意力头和MLP神经元，实现约79%的捷径攻击检测失败相对降低，同时保持正常检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全护栏（safety guards）被广泛用于过滤有害内容，通常通过监督微调训练。然而，训练数据中拒绝表达几乎仅与无害标签共现，这种数据偏斜导致模型可能学到虚假的"拒绝提示捷径"（refusal-cue shortcut），即在有害回复中插入拒绝语即可欺骗护栏。本文旨在审计并揭示这一系统性脆弱性，并提出轻量级缓解方案。

Method: 首先对WildGuardMix和GR-Train两个安全护栏训练数据集进行审计，发现拒绝表达与无害标签的共现偏斜。随后在LlamaGuard3、Qwen3Guard等模型上验证"拒绝提示捷径"攻击。为缓解此问题，提出稀疏互补掩码（sparse complementary masking）作为轻量级后处理干预：无需重新训练，通过识别并抑制一小部分与捷径相关的注意力头和MLP神经元来阻断捷径依赖，同时保留模型对真正拒绝表达的识别能力。

Result: 在两个主要基准测试上，该干预方法实现了约79%的由拒绝提示引起的响应初始检测失败的相对减少，同时保持了标准检测性能。尽管仅在单一响应位置优化掩码，抑制效果可迁移至未见过的位置和数据集。进一步分析表明，捷径依赖与合法拒绝识别在功能上部分可分离。

Conclusion: 安全护栏训练数据的标签偏斜导致了系统性的"拒绝提示捷径"脆弱性，影响多个已发布模型。稀疏互补掩码作为一种无需重训练的轻量级后处理干预，能有效抑制该捷径，且捷径相关组件与合法拒绝识别机制存在部分功能分离，为安全护栏的鲁棒性改进提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Safety guards are widely used to filter harmful content and are typically trained via supervised fine-tuning on labeled prompt-response pairs. We audit two widely used safety-guard training datasets, WildGuardMix and GR-Train, and find that among responses to harmful prompts, refusal expressions co-occur almost exclusively with unharmful labels. This imbalance motivates what we term the refusal-cue shortcut: inserting a refusal cue into a harmful response could flip the guard's verdict from harmful to unharmful. The shortcut affects not only guards trained on these datasets but also officially released models such as LlamaGuard3 and Qwen3Guard whose training data is undisclosed. It persists across response positions and is generally stronger in smaller variants within a family. To mitigate it, we adapt sparse complementary masking as a lightweight post-hoc intervention that identifies and suppresses a small set of shortcut-associated attention heads and MLP neurons without retraining. On two primary benchmarks, the intervention achieves an approximately 79% relative reduction in response-initial detection failures induced by refusal cues, while preserving standard detection performance. Although optimized using cues at a single response position, the suppression effect transfers to unseen positions and datasets, suggesting that shortcut manifestations across positions are partly mediated by shared internal components. Further analysis provides evidence that shortcut reliance and legitimate refusal recognition are partially functionally separable, as suppressing the shortcut broadly preserves the guard's ability to recognize genuine refusals.

</details>


### [240] [The Agent Operating System (AOS): A Reference Operating Architecture for Distributed Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03214)
*Ankur Sharma,Deep Shah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Agent Operating System (AOS)，一个供应商中立的分布式智能体系统参考操作架构。AOS包含控制与治理平面、运行时与协调平面两个内部平面，旨在将异构组件组合成可治理、可靠、可观测、可互操作的智能体系统，而非取代现有框架或基础设施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型已从孤立预测服务转变为长期运行的分布式系统组件，能够推理、调用工具、检索外部状态、委托任务并代表用户和组织行动。现有生态系统虽提供了智能体框架、工作流引擎、模型服务平台、记忆系统、通信协议和可观测性工具，但这些技术缺乏一个稳定且与实现无关的操作架构来治理意图、选择能力、在委托中保持权限、控制不确定性、协调运行时行为以及重建因果行为轨迹。

Method: 论文设计了AOS参考操作架构，包含两个内部平面：(1) 控制与治理平面——负责意图、策略、信任、权限、置信度、可审计性、可观测性和人工监督；(2) 运行时与协调平面——负责智能体生命周期、工作流协调、模型与工具路由、上下文与记忆协调、调度、流量管理和运行时保障。平台服务、操作系统、容器运行时和物理基础设施通过显式接口集成，位于AOS边界之外。论文还定义了AOS概念、不变量、接口对象、优化目标、部署配置和可靠性职责。

Result: AOS提供了一个与实现无关的架构蓝图，使得异构智能体组件可在统一的治理与运行时框架下组合运行。该架构明确了各层的职责边界、接口规范和可靠性分工，并识别了关键的权衡取舍与尚未解决的研究问题。

Conclusion: AOS并非要取代现有框架或基础设施，而是作为操作架构层，使异构组件能够组合成可治理、可靠、可观测、可互操作的智能体系统，填补了当前生态系统中治理与编排层面的架构空白。

Abstract: Large language models have transformed artificial intelligence from isolated prediction services into components of long-running, distributed systems that reason, invoke tools, retrieve external state, delegate tasks, and act on behalf of users and organizations. The surrounding ecosystem has responded with agent frameworks, workflow engines, model-serving platforms, memory systems, communication protocols, and observability tools. These technologies improve execution, but they do not provide a stable, implementation-independent operating architecture for governing intent, selecting capabilities, preserving authority across delegation, controlling uncertainty, coordinating runtime behavior, and reconstructing why consequential actions occurred. This paper proposes the Agent Operating System (AOS), a vendor-neutral reference operating architecture for distributed agentic systems. AOS contains two internal planes: a Control & Governance Plane responsible for intent, policy, trust, authority, confidence, auditability, observability, and human oversight; and a Runtime & Coordination Plane responsible for agent lifecycle, workflow coordination, model and tool routing, context and memory coordination, scheduling, traffic management, and runtime assurance. Platform services, Linux or Windows, container runtimes, and physical infrastructure remain outside the AOS boundary and are integrated through explicit interfaces. The paper specifies AOS concepts, invariants, interface objects, optimization objectives, deployment profiles, and reliability responsibilities. It also identifies tradeoffs and unresolved research questions. AOS is not presented as a replacement for existing frameworks or infrastructure; it is proposed as the operating architecture through which heterogeneous components can be composed into governable, reliable, observable, and interoperable agentic systems.

</details>


### [241] [Reachability Is Not Realization: Tracing the Sources of LLM Benchmark Gains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03219)
*Yanchao Li,Wanhao Liu,Jiaqing Xie,Ben Gao,Yanbo Wang,Tianfan Fu,Yuqiang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种问题级别的LLM审计框架，区分"实现"（模型默认输出正确答案）与"可达"（通过探测能找到正确答案），发现在多种设置下两者并非同步变化，许多基准提升实为可达性转化为实现，而非真正的能力扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM评估仅依赖聚合基准分数，无法区分模型能力的真正扩展（获得新知识）与已有能力的更好展现（将可达答案转化为实现答案）。作者旨在建立问题级别的精细审计方法，揭示基准分数提升背后的不同行为变化模式。

Method: 在固定预算、温度和答案格式下，定义"实现"为默认部署过程产生正确答案，"可达"为指定探测方法在固定预算内找到正确答案。方法包括：(1)测试推断时层路由对可达性的扩展效果；(2)通过沉默特定MLP块来修复未实现但可达的问题；(3)对比训练前后实现分数与可达上限的变化。

Result: 主要发现：(1)随机路由在43个模型-任务设置中匹配或超越结构化搜索，且答案盲过程几乎不保留增益；(2)沉默单个MLP块可修复68%-92%的预定义失败案例；(3)在六组评估中的五组，部署性能上升而可达上限不变或下降（如DAPO部署分数+14.7，可达上限-13.3）。

Conclusion: 实现与可达并非同步变化，许多基准提升仅是可达答案的"变现"而非真正能力扩展。建议能力声明应同时报告实现性能与可达性，并呼吁未来工作关注可达性上限以区分两类进步。

Abstract: Benchmark gains are often treated as evidence of greater LLM capability. Yet the same gain can reflect different changes in model behavior. A model may reach new answers, or produce answers that were already within reach. Aggregate scores do not distinguish these changes question by question. We establish a question-level audit under fixed budgets, temperatures, and answer formats. A question is realized when the default deployment procedure produces the correct answer. A question is reachable when a specified probe finds that answer within a fixed budget. We first test whether inference-time layer routing can expand reachability. Under a matched budget, random routes match or exceed structured search in all 43 model and task settings. Answer-blind procedures retain almost none of this gain, which instead requires access to the correct answer. We then ask why reachable answers sometimes fail to appear. Across six cases spanning 0.5B to 31B, silencing one identified MLP block repairs 68 to 92 percent of a predefined failure set. We next test whether training closes the gap by expanding reachability. In five of six matched evaluations, deployed performance rises while the reachable ceiling remains flat or falls. For DAPO, the deployed score rises by 14.7 points while the reachable ceiling falls by 13.3 points. Across the settings we audit, realization and reachability therefore do not always change together. Claims of capability expansion should report both realized performance and reachability under matched evaluation conditions. Code is available at https://github.com/LiZaiyuan0619/reachability-not-realization

</details>


### [242] [UniNav: A Unified World-Action Diffusion Model for Visual Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03244)
*Changqing Zhou,Yueru Luo,Zeyu Jiang,Changhao Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: UniNav 是一个统一的世界-动作模型，通过单一扩散过程同时生成未来视觉观测和连续路径点轨迹，在导航基准上实现了最优性能，且快速变体仅需0.1秒推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有导航策略能高效预测路径点但缺乏视觉前瞻能力，而世界模型能预测未来观测却需要昂贵的规划展开。两者功能互补但长期处于割裂状态，缺乏统一框架来同时享受两方优势。

Method: 提出 UniNav，基于单一 Transformer 扩散模型，将视觉 tokens 和路径点 tokens 联合去噪。引入几何感知的相机 tokens 增强空间定位能力，并在训练中同时利用带轨迹标注的导航数据和纯视频数据。设计两种变体：UniNav-Full 联合预测未来帧与轨迹，UniNav-Fast 在推理时去除未来图像 tokens 实现高效预测。

Result: 在导航基准上，UniNav 在所有数据集的 ATE（绝对轨迹误差）指标上均超越最强基线。UniNav-Fast 单步推理延迟仅 0.1 秒，且无明显精度下降。

Conclusion: UniNav 成功统一了未来预测与动作生成，兼顾可解释性和效率，为具身视觉导航提供了一种简洁且高性能的解决方案。

Abstract: Image-goal visual navigation is a fundamental capability for embodied agents. Existing navigation policies efficiently predict waypoint trajectories but lack visual foresight, while navigation world models can anticipate future observations but often require costly planning rollouts. We present UniNav, a unified world-action model that generates future visual observations and continuous waypoint trajectories through a single diffusion process. Given history frames and a goal image, UniNav jointly denoises visual and waypoint tokens within a single transformer, unifying future prediction and action generation in a shared framework. To improve spatial grounding, we incorporate geometry-aware camera tokens. We also train on both trajectory-labeled navigation data and video-only data, enabling the model to benefit from diverse videos without waypoint annotations. Based on this unified framework, we introduce two variants: UniNav-Full jointly predicts interpretable future observations and their corresponding trajectories, while UniNav-Fast removes future-image tokens at inference for efficient trajectory prediction. Experiments on navigation benchmarks show that UniNav outperforms the strongest baseline in ATE across all datasets. With one-step inference, UniNav-Fast achieves a latency of 0.1s without a substantial accuracy drop. Code will be released.

</details>


### [243] [One Knob to Rule Them All: A Unified Optimal Transport View of Cold-Start Active Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03249)
*Ning Zhu,Xiaochuan Ma,Juntao Xu,Jingze Liang,Mengfei Zhao,An Chen,Liang-Jian Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文重新审视冷启动主动学习（CSAL），通过最优传输理论提出统一框架，揭示现有方法的共享分配结构，建立极小极大界，并推导数据自适应正则化规则，最终提出ε-Adaptive Selection算法，在多个基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CSAL方法（基于典型性、覆盖性、多样性等）各自依赖不同的归纳偏置，在部分任务上表现良好但在其他任务上效果不佳。作者认为真正的挑战不在于设计又一种选择启发式，而在于使CSAL能够自动适应数据和任务。

Method: 1. 提出广义传输选择框架，统一现有方法的分配结构并精确包容代表性公式；2. 通过熵正则化建立理论分析，刻画其控制的权衡，并给出任务无关的冷启动选择极小极大界；3. 推导数据自适应正则化规则，基于Sinkhorn算法实现ε-Adaptive Selection（ε-AS）。

Result: 在六个公开数据集和多种标注预算下，ε-AS一致达到SOTA性能；在ImageNet-1k上，相比ActiveFT平均准确率提升1.29%，同时选择时间减少56.2%。

Conclusion: 通过最优传输视角统一CSAL方法，结合熵正则化的理论分析，实现了数据自适应的冷启动主动学习，在效果和效率上均优于现有方法。

Abstract: Cold-Start Active Learning (CSAL) aims to select a valuable subset from an unlabeled pool without any prior knowledge or human assistance. Existing methods take diverse routes based on typicality, coverage, or diversity. Each rests on its own inductive bias and therefore performs well on some tasks yet poorly on others. We argue that the real challenge is not to design yet another selection heuristic, but to make CSAL adapt automatically to the data and task at hand. To this end, we revisit CSAL through the lens of optimal transport. First, we propose a generalized transport selection framework that reveals the shared allocation structure of existing methods and exactly subsumes representative formulations. Second, we introduce a theoretical analysis that characterizes the trade-off controlled by entropic regularization and establishes a task-agnostic minimax bound for cold-start selection. These results provide a principled foundation for adapting the regularization strength to the unlabeled data. Third, we derive a data-adaptive regularization rule and present a novel Sinkhorn-based CSAL algorithm, termed $ε$-Adaptive Selection ($ε$-AS). Extensive experiments on six public datasets and multiple annotation budgets show that $ε$-AS consistently achieves state-of-the-art performance. On ImageNet-1k, it improves the average accuracy over ActiveFT by 1.29% while reducing selection time by 56.2%. Code will be released at https://github.com/Z-yiwei/OT-CSAL

</details>


### [244] [TaskPress: Query-Agnostic KV Cache Compression via Task-Guided Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03276)
*Wonpyo Park,Seung-won Hwang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TaskPress是一种任务引导的、与查询无关的KV缓存驱逐框架，通过高层任务指南构建可复用的紧凑内存表示，并利用量化缩放因子作为零成本异常检测信号来识别重要token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KV缓存剪枝方法针对特定查询确定token重要性，无法跨未见查询复用；同时KV缓存随序列长度线性增长，制约了长上下文大语言模型推理效率。因此需要一种与查询无关、可复用的缓存压缩方案。

Method: 提出TaskPress框架：(1) 以高层任务指南作为"元查询"，在预填充阶段过滤与任务无关的token，构建可跨查询复用的紧凑内存表示；(2) 利用量化缩放因子作为零成本信号检测有影响力的表示异常值，作为token重要性的高效代理指标。

Result: 在多种长上下文任务上的实验表明，TaskPress能够高效地创建紧凑且可跨多样化查询复用的KV缓存，验证了其有效性和通用性。

Conclusion: TaskPress通过任务引导的查询无关缓存驱逐策略，实现了长上下文场景下高效且可复用的KV缓存压缩，为LLM推理优化提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Long-context inference with large language models is constrained by the linear growth of the key-value cache to sequence length. While pruning offers mitigation, prevailing methods determine query-specific token importance that cannot be reused across unseen queries. In contrast, we introduce TaskPress, a framework for task-guided, query-agnostic KV cache eviction. Instead of optimizing the cache for a single query, TaskPress constructs a reusable memory representation conditioned on a high-level task guide. The guide functions as a meta-query during prefill to filter irrelevant tokens before downstream queries are issued. In addition, TaskPress leverages quantization scale factors as a zero-cost signal for detecting influential representation outliers, providing an efficient proxy for token importance. Experiments on conducted on various tasks with long context input demonstrate that TaskPress efficiently creates a compact, reusable cache across diverse queries.

</details>


### [245] [AgentPanel: Toward a New Paradigm for Human--AI Collaboration in Exploring Scientific Questions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03283)
*Zhiyao Cui,Qianyi Wang,Haoyang Yan,Yiqun Zhang,Siyue Ren,Hangfan Zhang,Zelin Tan,Hao Li,Chunjiang Mu,Dexian Cai,Shao Zhang,Chen Zhang,Meng Li,Jianan Chai,Yuting Fan,Zichao Ye,Xiaolei Yang,Xinyao Lu,Yuyang Yu,Wenjie Lou,Xiaosong Wang,Fenghua Ling,Shiyang Feng,Mao Su,Qiaosheng Zhang,Bo Zhang,Yang Chen,Lei Bai,Shuyue Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgentPanel 是一个多智能体论坛平台，通过异质智能体异步讨论科学问题来辅助研究人员进行科学探索，在观点多样性、探索广度方面优于集中式多智能体辩论基线，65%的参与者认可其在早期探索中的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前科研实践中识别有前景的科学想法依赖小组讨论或单一大语言模型交互，这些方式仅能暴露有限的观点和方向，缺乏多样化的视角支持。

Method: 提出 AgentPanel，一个论坛式多智能体人机协作平台。异质智能体在论坛环境中异步讨论科学问题；研究人员可提交问题、浏览整理候选想法、与智能体进行后续交互，并可选择生成事后总结报告。通过离线实验和20人用户研究进行评估。

Result: 离线实验中，AgentPanel 在想法质量、探索广度、交互有效性和候选选择效率上均优于集中式多智能体辩论基线。用户研究表明参与者看重其视角多样性和探索支持；在与常用 LLM 工具的体验对比中，65% 的参与者在研究方向广度和早期探索整体适用性上更青睐 AgentPanel。

Conclusion: AgentPanel 通过论坛式多智能体异步讨论有效扩展了科学探索的视角多样性，为人机协作科研探索提供了一种有前景的新范式。平台已公开发布。

Abstract: Identifying promising scientific ideas remains an important challenge in research practice. Researchers commonly rely on small-group discussions or one-to-one interactions with a single large language model, yet these approaches often expose them to only a limited range of perspectives and directions. We present AgentPanel, a multi-agent forum for human--AI collaboration in scientific exploration. Heterogeneous agents asynchronously discuss scientific questions in a forum-style environment, while researchers can submit questions, browse and organize candidate ideas, engage agents in follow-up interactions, and optionally generate post-hoc summary reports. We evaluate AgentPanel in terms of idea quality, exploration breadth, interaction effectiveness, candidate-selection efficiency, and practical utility. Offline experiments show that AgentPanel outperforms a centralized multi-agent debate baseline. A human study with 20 participants further shows that users value AgentPanel for perspective diversity and exploration support. In experience-based comparisons with commonly used LLM tools, 65\% of participants favored AgentPanel for both breadth of research directions and overall suitability for early-stage exploration. The platform is publicly available at https://agentpanel.cc/.

</details>


### [246] [DocTrace: Towards Traceable Long Document VQA via Hierarchical Evidence Graph Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03292)
*Le Xiang,Zhicheng Guan,Hong Chen,Xiaocong Lin,Zhenghua Lei,Teng Hu,Bolei He,Long Zeng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DocTrace 将长文档视觉问答建模为显式证据图推理问题，通过层次化框架逐步完成证据定位、结构化文档解析和证据图推理，结合 SFT 与 GRPO 两阶段训练，在三个基准上大幅超越现有开源和闭源模型，同时提供可追溯的证据来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长文档 VQA 方法（端到端 MLLM、RAG 管线、文档智能体）缺乏对推理过程中证据逐步组合的显式表示与验证机制，导致答案准确性和可追溯性不足。

Method: 提出 DocTrace 层次化框架：(1) 显式证据图推理替代隐式答案预测；(2) 两阶段训练——联合 SFT 初始化证据定位与图推理能力，再通过任务特定的 GRPO 结合专用奖励函数进一步优化。

Result: 以 Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct 为骨干，DocTrace 在 MMLongBench-Doc、LongDocURL、SlideVQA 上分别取得 14.4、11.3、11.7 个百分点的绝对提升，一致超越开源基线及闭源 MLLM。

Conclusion: DocTrace 不仅实现了竞争性的问答性能，更重要的是构建了具备节点级来源的可追溯证据图，为长文档理解提供了透明、可验证的推理路径。

Abstract: Long Document Visual Question Answering (LongDocVQA) requires Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) to locate, integrate, and reason over heterogeneous document elements distributed across multiple pages. Existing approaches, including end-to-end MLLMs, retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) pipelines, and document agents, often lack explicit mechanisms to represent and verify how grounded evidence is progressively composed during reasoning, limiting both answer accuracy and traceability. In this paper, we cast LongDocVQA as an explicit evidence graph reasoning problem rather than implicit answer prediction. To this end, we propose DocTrace, a hierarchical framework that progressively performs evidence localization, structured document parsing, and evidence graph reasoning to enable explicit evidence provenance. To effectively learn these capabilities, we develop a two-stage training framework: joint Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) first initializes evidence localization and graph reasoning abilities, followed by task-specific Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with dedicated rewards to further optimize these capabilities. Extensive experiments on MMLongBench-Doc, LongDocURL, and SlideVQA demonstrate that DocTrace consistently outperforms both existing open-source baselines and proprietary MLLMs. Compared with the Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct backbone, DocTrace achieves absolute improvements of 14.4, 11.3, and 11.7 points on the three benchmarks, respectively. Beyond competitive performance, DocTrace constructs traceable evidence graphs with explicit node-level provenance, enabling transparent and verifiable reasoning for long document understanding.

</details>


### [247] [Distractor-Aware Truncation: Disentangling Context-Length Effects from Signal Loss in Long-Context LLM Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03297)
*Mohsen Arjmandi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究挑战了"短上下文更好"的标准假设，通过对比两种截断协议（朴素中间删除 vs. 保留任务相关内容的干扰感知删除），揭示所谓"上下文缩短导致性能下降"实为答案内容被误删的产物，而非上下文窗口本身的效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验检索增强/记忆增强语言模型文献中的一个标准主张——在保留相关信息的前提下，更短的上下文更好。作者质疑先前研究中使用的朴素截断协议是否混淆了"上下文长度效应"与"信号保留率"两个变量。

Method: 在两个长上下文基准（BABILong、GraphWalks-BFS）上，对每个样本以四种保留比例（100%、75%、50%、25%）运行两种截断协议：（1）朴素协议——从提示中部删除内容；（2）干扰感知协议——识别并仅保留任务相关内容。评估Claude系列三款模型（Haiku 4.5、Sonnet 4.6、Opus 4.7）及GPT-5.5，并在MRCR v2和Oolong基准上复现。使用配对Wilcoxon检验与Holm校正。

Result: 朴素截断下，所有BABILong和GraphWalks单元的得分单调崩溃（p_adj < 0.05）。干扰感知协议下，性能保持不变或提升：两款较小Claude模型在BABILong上取得统计显著增益，较大模型（Opus 4.7、GPT-5.5）维持全上下文天花板水平。GPT-5.5复现相同模式，排除供应商特例。机制分析表明，朴素协议在25%保留率下仅不到1%的样本保留了答案内容，而干扰感知协议通过构造保证了信号保留。

Conclusion: 朴素截断协议测量的并非上下文窗口效应，而是中间删除偶然保留答案的概率。未来研究上下文长度效应时，必须明确说明如何区分信号与干扰项，否则结果将在两种对立的假设之间无法解释。

Abstract: A standard claim in the literature on retrieval-augmented and memory-augmented language models is that shorter context is better when the relevant information is preserved. We test this claim by running every sample of two long-context benchmarks -- BABILong and GraphWalks (BFS) -- at four context-retention fractions (100%, 75%, 50%, 25%) under two truncation protocols. The first is the naive protocol implicitly used in much prior work: drop content from the middle of the prompt. The second is distractor-aware: identify the task-relevant content for each sample and drop only the rest. We evaluate three sizes of the Claude family (Haiku 4.5, Sonnet 4.6, Opus 4.7) and, to test cross-provider generality, GPT-5.5 from a different provider; we apply the same protocol to two further benchmarks (MRCR v2, Oolong). Under naive truncation, score collapses monotonically (paired Wilcoxon, Holm-corrected p_adj < 0.05 in all eight BABILong and GraphWalks cells). Under the distractor-aware protocol -- which preserves the signal by construction -- performance is preserved or improves: the two smaller Claude models show statistically significant gains on BABILong, while the larger models (Opus 4.7 and GPT-5.5) sit at their full-context ceiling. The naive collapse and its distractor-aware recovery replicate on GPT-5.5, ruling out a single-provider artifact. The mechanism is direct: under the naive protocol the answer-bearing content survives in fewer than 1% of samples at 25% retention; under the distractor-aware protocol it is preserved by construction. The naive protocol is therefore not a measurement of context-window effects; it is a measurement of how often middle-removal happens to spare the answer. We conclude that future studies of context-length effects must specify how they distinguish signal from distractor, or they are at best ambiguous between two opposite hypotheses.

</details>


### [248] [SeaSlides: Semantic Abstraction Layer for Agentic Slide Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03298)
*Shengjun Fang,Chenyang Wu,Zongzhang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SeaSlides 提出一种基于语义抽象层的智能体幻灯片生成框架，将内容创作与布局/渲染解耦。通过可复用组件和能力模块（公式、代码、图表等），在 HTML 和 Typst 两种后端上实现了结构化、可维护的演示文稿生成，在富内容基准测试中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有幻灯片生成系统存在根本性矛盾：模板系统保布局规整但限制适应性，自由格式（HTML/SVG）提供灵活性但要求模型处理底层渲染细节，导致长篇幅技术演示文稿（含公式、代码、图表等）脆弱易错。需要一种兼顾内容保真、视觉一致性和渲染专业性的方案。

Method: 提出 SeaSlides 框架，核心是语义抽象层：模型仅撰写结构化幻灯片内容（通过可复用组件和能力模块），模板负责布局、样式与渲染。分别以 HTML 和 Typst 实例化：HTML 版使用模板定义 DOM 组件，Typst 版使用模板函数与包支持模块。能力模块将公式、代码、图表路由至专用渲染器。三道反馈阶段在导出前依次定位构建错误、项目约束违规和视觉缺陷。

Result: 在 128 任务的 UltraPresent 验证集和新构建的 32 任务 SeaSlidesBench-Rich（侧重数学、代码、伪代码、表格、图表、示意图）上，使用四种生成模型评估。两种 SeaSlides 后端均生成比 SVG 重度方案更具可读性的内容导向源码。在四种模型中的三种上，SeaSlides 后端取得最高富内容宏平均分，同时整体定性性能保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 语义抽象是跨演示文稿后端的实用创作原则，能有效提升富内容技术幻灯片生成的可读性与质量。

Abstract: Agentic presentation generation must preserve source content, maintain coherent visual design, render specialized objects, and produce usable artifacts. Existing systems meet only part of this requirement: templates preserve regularity but restrict adaptation, whereas free-form HTML or SVG gives models flexibility at the cost of low-level rendering decisions. This mismatch makes long technical decks brittle, especially when slides contain formulas, code, or data graphics. We present SeaSlides, an agentic slide-generation framework built around a semantic abstraction layer. Rather than authoring coordinates, inline styles, or raw SVG geometry, the model writes structured slide content through reusable components and capability modules, while templates own layout, style, and rendering. We instantiate this principle separately in HTML and Typst: SeaSlides-HTML uses template-defined DOM components, whereas SeaSlides-Typst uses template functions and package-backed modules. Capability modules route equations, code, and charts to dedicated renderers, and three feedback stages localize build errors, project-constraint violations, and visual defects before export. The two systems retain backend-specific syntax and contracts while sharing the same authoring boundary. For evaluation, we combine the 128-task UltraPresent validation setting with SeaSlidesBench-Rich, a new 32-task benchmark stressing mathematics, code, pseudocode, tables, charts, and diagrams. Across four generation models, both SeaSlides backends produce more readable, content-oriented source than SVG-heavy generation. A SeaSlides backend attains the highest rich-content macro-average under three of the four models while maintaining competitive overall qualitative performance. These results support semantic abstraction as a practical authoring principle across presentation backends.

</details>


### [249] [Screenshots or Tools? Eliciting Tool Use and Managing Multimodal Context in Hybrid GUI-MCP Computer-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03327)
*Siqi Fan,Minghao Li,Xiaoqian Ma,Wenhui Tan,Xiusheng Huang,Juntong Wu,Liujie Zhang,Shuo Shang,Weihang Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 混合型计算机使用Agent可同时通过截图和文本工具操作。研究发现，相同MCP工具对推理模型提升+4.0pp，却使非推理模型下降-5.9pp，关键在于工具决策行为。非推理模型存在忽视、误命名、虚假终止等工具使用失败；推理模型虽避免这些问题，但仅在23.9%的可达工具任务中实际调用工具（"采用差距"）。通过多轮RL，工具采用率可从0.03提升至0.33，但准确率未跟随——行为可引导，能力不可。此外，成功工具调用常使后续截图冗余，压缩图像历史可将输入token降低约1/3，且在重训练后消除精度损失，最终以53%的输入成本达到37.8%准确率（对比未压缩的33.0%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前混合型计算机使用Agent中，工具可用性对模型性能的影响方向不一致——同一工具有时提升、有时损害性能。研究者试图揭示这一现象背后的根本原因，并探索如何弥合工具"可用"与"实际使用"之间的差距（即"采用差距"），从而提升Agent的工具利用效率。

Method: 在GUI-MCP框架下的OSWorld-MCP基准（309个任务）上，对比推理模型与非推理模型在相同MCP工具下的表现（各5次运行）。分析两类模型的工具决策行为差异（忽视、误命名、虚假终止等失败模式）。通过多轮强化学习（RL）施加稠密工具奖励来提升工具采用率，并设计图像历史压缩策略（丢弃工具调用成功后的冗余截图），在重训练下验证压缩Agent的精度-成本权衡。

Result: （1）相同MCP工具使推理模型+4.0pp，非推理模型-5.9pp，差异源于工具决策行为。（2）推理模型仅在23.9%的可达任务中调用工具，其余形成"采用差距"。（3）动作级RL：稠密工具奖励使电子表格工具采用率从0.03升至0.33，但留出准确率未提升——行为可引导，能力不可。（4）上下文级压缩：丢弃工具调用后的冗余截图可削减约1/3输入token，重训练后压缩Agent以53%输入成本达37.8%准确率（vs未压缩33.0%），并消除"丰富-精简"子集间的性能差距。

Conclusion: 工具对Agent的帮助取决于模型是否能够正确选择并整合工具，当前混合Agent在此方面存在大量未利用的选择空间。核心瓶颈在于工具调用语义（tool-call semantics），而非行为引导。通过上下文压缩与重训练，可在大幅降低计算成本的同时提升性能，揭示了改进混合Agent的可行路径。

Abstract: Hybrid computer-use agents can act through screenshots or call text tools. We find that having a tool available does not settle which way the effect goes. Under one identical GUI-MCP harness on the OSWorld-MCP benchmark (309 tasks), the same MCP tools improve a reasoning model by +4.0pp and degrade a non-reasoning model by -5.9pp (5 runs each, both beyond 2 SE). What separates the two is tool-decision behavior. The non-reasoning policy ignores, misnames, or falsely terminates around tools. The reasoning model avoids these failures, yet still calls a tool on only 55/309 tasks, 23.9% of the tool-reachable ones. We call this shortfall the adoption gap. Both levels of the problem share one cause: the model already has a cheaper route and is never trained to take it. Multi-turn RL probes that cause. At the action level, a dense tool bonus raises spreadsheet adoption 0.03 -> 0.33 and carries into greedy decoding, but held-out accuracy does not follow. Behavior is steerable; competence is not. The bottleneck lies in tool-call semantics. At the context level, a successful tool call often makes the next screenshot redundant. Dropping it and halving image history cuts input tokens by about a third, at a small accuracy cost. Retraining under the same observation rule removes that cost. The compressed agent then reaches 37.8% against 33.0% for the uncompressed operating point, at 53% of the input cost, and closes the rich-lean gap on a pre-registered degraded subset to zero. Tools help when the model chooses and integrates them, and current hybrid agents leave many such choices unused.

</details>


### [250] [Long-term Traffic Scene Prediction via Polynomial Representations in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03330)
*Yue Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该博士论文提出基于多项式表示的交通场景预测方法，证明多项式表示在计算效率、泛化能力和预测合理性方面优于传统序列表示，并在Argoverse 2和Waymo Open数据集上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于序列的交通场景预测表示方法对噪声敏感且泛化能力不足，无法充分满足自动驾驶对计算效率和预测合理性的需求。论文旨在引入多项式表示作为更高效、更具泛化性的替代方案。

Method: 采用中等次数多项式对轨迹和地图几何进行统一表示，构建预测模型；进一步扩展为基于扩散的生成式框架，实现多智能体场景生成。通过理论分析与实证验证相结合的方式进行评估。

Result: 在Argoverse 2和Waymo Open数据集上，多项式表示模型在标准基准上达到接近最优精度，同时在分布偏移下泛化能力显著提升；扩散生成框架产生更合理且运动学一致的交通延续；多项式表示降低了计算成本，提升了跨数据集泛化能力和轨迹平滑性。

Conclusion: 多项式轨迹表示是安全关键型自动驾驶中交通场景预测的高效、表达力强且可泛化的基础方法；标准分布内评估和回归类指标可能无法反映模型的真实泛化能力和预测合理性。

Abstract: This thesis addresses fundamental challenges in traffic scene prediction for autonomous driving by introducing robust and computationally efficient models based on polynomial representations. While conventional sequence-based representations often struggle with noise and generalization, this work demonstrates that polynomial representations offer significant advantages in computational efficiency, generalization, and prediction plausibility. Through theoretical analysis and empirical validation, this thesis demonstrates that moderate-degree polynomials capture real-world motion dynamics with high fidelity without constraining predictive performance. Building on this foundation, a prediction model representing both trajectories and map geometry with polynomial representations achieves near state-of-the-art accuracy on standard benchmarks while substantially improving generalization under distribution shift. Extending this concept, a diffusion- based generative framework enables multi-agent scene generation, producing traffic continuations that are more plausible and kinematically consistent than those generated by conventional baselines. Evaluations on the Argoverse 2 and Waymo Open datasets confirm that polynomial representations reduce computational cost, enhance cross-dataset generalization, and yield smoother trajectories and higher behavioral plausibility. The findings reveal that standard in-distribution evaluation and regression-based metrics may fail to reflect true model generalization and prediction plausibility. By providing theoretical justification and empirical validation, this dissertation estab- lishes polynomial trajectory representations as an efficient, expressive, and generalizable foundation for traffic scene prediction in safety critical autonomous driving.

</details>


### [251] [Traceable Multi-Agent System for Knowledge-Based Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03339)
*Junhyeok Kang,Sangjun Han,Hyeokjun Choe,Soonyoung Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TraceMAS 是一个面向可追溯多智能体预测的交互式演示系统，通过理想因果环路图（Ideal CLD）和数据锚定因果环路图（Data-Grounded CLD）两种表示，将自主预测智能体的文档解读、数据搜索、代码生成和模型修订过程组织为可检查的证据链，并以原油价格预测为例展示其功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业预测日益依赖自主智能体进行文档解读、数据检索、代码生成与模型修订，但自主性增强的同时导致预测变更的原因、支撑证据以及数据和建模选择如何被修订变得难以追溯和检查。

Method: TraceMAS 围绕两种因果环路表示组织智能体输出：(1) 理想因果环路图（Ideal CLD），从领域文档中提取关键因素及其因果关系；(2) 数据锚定因果环路图（Data-Grounded CLD），将上述因素链接到内部变量、外部数据或已记录的代理变量，并以此指导特征构建与模型设计，保持文本证据、数据选择与模型修订之间的关联。

Result: 在原油价格预测场景中，TraceMAS 演示界面支持用户比较预测迭代、检查智能体级别的修订、探索因果图谱、审查特征-数据映射与模型架构，以及将情景预测与市场叙事相连接。

Conclusion: 该演示表明，自主预测智能体可以在保持灵活性的同时，使从证据到预测的全过程具备可检查性。

Abstract: Enterprise forecasting increasingly relies on autonomous agents that interpret documents, search for data, generate code, and revise models. While this autonomy helps build adaptive forecasting pipelines, it also makes it difficult for practitioners to inspect why a forecast changed, which evidence supported the change, and how data and modeling choices were revised. We present TraceMAS, an interactive demo system for traceable multi-agent forecasting. TraceMAS organizes agent outputs around two causal-loop representations: an Ideal Causal Loop Diagram (Ideal CLD), which captures key factors and their causal relations extracted from domain documents, and a Data-Grounded Causal Loop Diagram (Data-Grounded CLD), which links those factors to internal variables, external data, or documented proxies. The Data-Grounded CLD guides feature construction and model design while preserving the connection between textual evidence, data choices, and model revisions. We demonstrate TraceMAS on crude oil price forecasting. The demo interface allows users to compare forecasting iterations, inspect agent-level revisions, explore causal maps, review feature-data mappings and model architecture, and connect scenario forecasts to market narratives. This demonstration shows how autonomous forecasting agents can retain flexibility while making the evidence-to-forecast process inspectable.

</details>


### [252] [MMLongBench-Doc-V2: A Corrected-Annotation, Semantics-Aware Revision of MMLongBench-Doc](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03397)
*Mingtian Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MMLongBench-Doc-V2 对原基准进行了系统修正：纠正了106个错误/模糊标注，将字符串匹配指标替换为基于LLM的语义评判，移除了10个文件名错误和1个重复问题，并提供了空集键是否可放宽的决策程序。修正后得分与V1不可直接比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原MMLongBench-Doc基准存在两个核心问题：(1) 基于字符串精确匹配的评估指标导致语义等价的数值格式差异被误判为错误（如1,358,000 vs 1358000）；(2) 相当比例的真实标注存在错误、模糊或不完整，且这些标注缺陷恰好集中在强模型能正确回答的问题上，系统性地压低了能力分数。这导致评估结果偏离了其本应衡量的真实能力。

Method: 作者采取了四项修正措施：(1) 发布了逐条修正记录，包含106个标注修正，并附上定位页面和验证算术；(2) 将字符串匹配指标替换为固定的LLM评判器，判断回答是否与参考答案语义一致；(3) 移除10个文档文件名错误对应的问题和1个重复问题，最终保留1,071题/134篇文档；(4) 提出空集键放宽的决策程序——判断何时可安全扩大空集键的范围、何时扩大会破坏刻意构造的负样本，应用于全部208行，最终放宽了14行。

Result: 修正后的MMLongBench-Doc-V2包含1,071个问题、134篇文档，修正了106个标注错误，放宽了14个空集键。修正后的评分体系与已发表的V1分数不可直接比较。所有修正语料、逐条修正记录和评估框架已开源发布。

Conclusion: MMLongBench-Doc-V2通过修正标注错误和替换评估指标，显著提升了基准的可靠性和信度，使其能更准确地衡量长文档QA系统的真实能力。其中空集键放宽的决策程序是最具复用价值的贡献，可作为其他基准维护的通用方法。

Abstract: MMLongBench-Doc is a long-document QA benchmark of 1,082 questions over 135 PDFs. Two properties of it push measured scores away from the quantity they are meant to capture: the reference metric compares extracted answers, so 1,358,000 loses to 1358000; and a non-trivial share of ground-truth annotations are wrong, ambiguous, or incomplete --- concentrated, because of how they were found, in exactly the questions capable systems answer correctly. MMLongBench-Doc-V2 corrects 106 annotations, each published with the page and arithmetic that settle it, and replaces the string metric with a pinned LLM judge asked whether a response means the reference. Ten questions whose document ships under the wrong filename are removed rather than counted wrong, along with one duplicated question, leaving 1,071 questions over 134 documents. The most reusable contribution is a decision procedure for when an empty set key may be widened and when widening would destroy a deliberate negative sample; applied to all 208 rows, it widened 14. V2 scores are not comparable with published V1 numbers. The corrected corpus, the per-entry correction record and the evaluation harness are available at https://github.com/VectifyAI/MMLongBench-Doc-V2.

</details>


### [253] [Towards Robust Tool Use in Agents via Experience-Driven Adaptive Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03403)
*Can Wang,Haoran Chen,Li Yu,Ding Hao,Bohai Zhao,Zhaoyang Liu,Zhiying Tu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ExpG 提出一种三阶段经验引导机制（经验获取、经验蒸馏、经验复用），通过从历史执行轨迹中提取并精炼工具使用的最佳实践，显著提升智能体在工具选择、工具调用和响应生成任务中的鲁棒性，使小模型智能体在鲁棒性上超越不使用 ExpG 的大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前智能体的性能瓶颈正从模型能力转向执行过程的鲁棒性，而工具作为智能体与外部环境交互的核心接口，现有方法很少关注如何在多样化运行时条件下确保鲁棒的工具体验。论文正是针对这一空白，旨在系统性地提升工具使用的鲁棒性。

Method: ExpG 包含三个阶段：(1) 经验获取——从历史执行轨迹中通过多维度归因分析工具调用质量，生成结构化的可学习经验；(2) 经验蒸馏——通过过滤无益经验、基于等价类方法筛选代表性经验，并将其总结为可泛化的指导，保持经验池的有效性；(3) 经验复用——在未来任务求解过程中自适应地应用这些指导。

Result: 大量实验表明，ExpG 在工具选择、工具调用和响应生成三个任务上均带来一致性的性能提升，使小规模智能体能够超越不使用 ExpG 的大规模智能体，且在挑战性场景下增益尤为显著。

Conclusion: ExpG 为提升智能体工具使用的鲁棒性提供了一条有前景的路径，表明通过系统性地从历史经验中学习和复用指导，可以在不增大模型规模的情况下显著改善执行过程的可靠性。

Abstract: The performance bottleneck of agents is increasingly shifting from model capability to the robustness of their execution processes. Tools play a central role as the primary interface through which agents interact with external environments, yet existing methods rarely focus on ensuring robust tool use across diverse runtime conditions. To address this problem, we propose ExpG, a mechanism that builds and refines adaptive guidance capturing each tool's capability boundaries and best practices, thereby enabling agents to use tools more robustly and effectively. ExpG consists of three phases: (1) experience acquisition, which analyzes tool invocation quality from historical execution trajectories, producing structured learnable experiences through multi-aspect attribution; (2) experience distillation, which keeps the experience pool effective by filtering unhelpful experiences, selecting representative ones with an equivalence-class-based method, and summarizing them into generalizable guidance; and (3) experience reuse, which applies the guidance adaptively during future task solving. Extensive experiments show that ExpG brings consistent improvements across the tool selection, tool calling, and response generation tasks, enabling smaller agents to outperform larger ones that do not use ExpG. Moreover, ExpG achieves particularly strong gains in challenging settings, suggesting a promising path toward more robust tool use. Our code, experiments, and results are available.

</details>


<div id='stat.ME'></div>

# stat.ME [[Back]](#toc)

### [254] [Conformal confidence intervals with an application to small area estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02766)
*Li-Chun Zhang,Tiziana Tuoto*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的共形推断方法，用于构建样本内结果未知期望的置信区间，其覆盖概率以已实现样本为条件。该方法填补了经典回归与共形推断之间的空白，并应用于小域估计问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有共形预测推断能够为样本外结果提供有限样本下的有效覆盖保证，但针对样本内结果的未知期望（即回归函数的条件期望），目前缺乏具有条件覆盖保证的置信区间构造方法。经典回归依赖模型假设，而共形推断通常关注预测区间而非期望的置信区间，两者之间存在方法学空白。

Method: 作者提出一种新的共形置信区间构造方法。该方法基于可交换性或独立同分布随机变量假设，利用共形推断框架，针对已实现样本条件下样本内结果的未知期望，构建具有指定覆盖概率的置信区间。核心在于将共形推断从预测域扩展到期望估计域，实现在有限样本下对条件期望的覆盖保证。

Result: 该方法能够在有限样本量且回归函数存在不可避免的误设情况下，仍然实现对样本内结果未知期望的有效覆盖，覆盖概率以已实现样本为条件得到保证。

Conclusion: 所提出的共形置信区间成功弥合了经典回归与共形推断之间的鸿沟，为回归分析中的期望推断提供了有限样本下的频率学覆盖保证，并在小域估计等实际应用中展现出价值。

Abstract: Conformal prediction inference yields intervals for out-of-sample random outcomes with designated coverage probabilities, given exchangeable or independent-and-identically distributed random variables. For regression analysis, valid coverage can be achieved given finite sample sizes, despite unavoidable misspecifications of the regression function. We propose a novel method of conformal inference, aimed to produce confidence intervals of the unknown expectations of the in-sample outcomes with the designated coverage probabilities conditional on the realised sample. These conformal confidence intervals fill a gap between classical regression and conformal inference. The proposed approach is applied to small area estimation problems.

</details>


### [255] [DAIF: A Data-Driven Intermediate Fusion Framework for Multimodal Supervised Learning via Approximate Message Passing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02769)
*Sagnik Nandy,Samriddha Lahiry,Pragya Sur,Subhabrata Sen*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出DAIF（数据自适应中间融合）框架，利用随机矩阵理论和非参数依赖性度量从数据中自动学习多模态融合结构。该方法基于贝叶斯多模态因子模型，通过聚类模态并使用经验贝叶斯估计先验，在近似消息传递框架内构建去噪器，生成可用于下游预测的低维特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态监督学习中，融合粒度的选择至关重要：过度融合会放大噪声，融合不足则无法利用跨模态依赖性。现有方法依赖预先指定的融合架构（如早融合、晚融合），难以适应模态间真实的依赖结构。

Method: 提出DAIF框架：基于贝叶斯多模态因子模型，先验决定跨模态依赖。首先通过估计的模态间依赖性对模态进行聚类，然后进行聚类级别经验贝叶斯先验估计；利用估计的先验在近似消息传递（AMP）框架内构建去噪器，生成既跨相关模态借力又保留模态特异性信号的低维嵌入，用于下游监督预测。

Result: 在多种依赖结构和信号状态下的模拟评估中，DAIF与多个基准方法进行比较；在TEA-seq三模态数据集（预测T细胞分化标记蛋白表达）和TCGA-BRCA数据集（患者生存预测）两个实际应用中，DAIF达到或超越最先进技术。

Conclusion: DAIF作为一种数据自适应的中间融合方法，能够根据数据内在结构自动确定融合策略，在多种监督学习任务中表现出竞争性或优越性能，证明其跨不同预测任务的通用性。

Abstract: Multimodal supervised learning seeks to leverage multiple heterogeneous data sources to improve predictive performance. A central challenge is determining the fusion granularity across modalities: over-integration may amplify noise while under-integration fails to exploit cross-modal dependence. Existing approaches rely on pre-specified fusion architectures, from early to late fusion, that may not adapt to the underlying dependence structure among modalities. We propose DAIF, a data adaptive intermediate fusion framework that combines random matrix theory and non-parametric dependence measures to learn fusion structure directly from data. We operate under a Bayesian multimodal factor model where the prior on the latent factors determines the cross-modal dependence. Our method clusters modalities based on estimated intermodal dependence, then performs clusterwise empirical Bayes estimation of the priors. These estimated priors are used to construct denoisers within an approximate message passing (AMP) framework, yielding denoised low-dimensional features that borrow strength across related modalities while preserving modality-specific signal. The resulting embeddings are used for downstream supervised prediction. We evaluate the framework through simulations under varying dependence structures and signal regimes, comparing against several benchmark methods, and demonstrate its practical utility on two multimodal datasets, namely a trimodal TEA-seq dataset (Swanson et al., 2021) and TCGA-BRCA dataset (Goldman et al., 2020). In the first example, we predict the expression level of a T-cell differentiation marker protein and in the second case we analyze patient survival prediction based on multimodal information. Our method competes with or outperforms the state-of-the-art techniques in both prediction problems, demonstrating its versatility across diverse supervised learning tasks.

</details>


### [256] [Estimating Climate Sensitivity Using Bayesian Model Averaging for CMIP Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02923)
*Jitong Jiang,Skylar Shi,Adrian E. Raftery*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于贝叶斯模型平均（BMA）的数据驱动方法，利用37个CMIP6气候模型并结合观测温度数据进行加权，重新估算累积CO₂排放的瞬态气候响应（TCRE）。相比IPCC AR6的估计，该方法得出更高的均值和更低的标准差，预测的2100年升温幅度略高且不确定性更小，并通过样本外验证和方差分解证明了方法的良好校准性和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TCRE估算常依赖主观模型筛选或预设的气候敏感性范围，且缺乏对观测数据的充分验证，亟需一种完全基于统计、可验证且能分解不确定性来源的客观估算框架。

Method: 采用贝叶斯模型平均（BMA）方法，整合CMIP6的37个气候模型，依据各模型模拟与历史观测温度数据的一致性进行加权；通过样本外预测验证评估校准效果，并利用方差分解量化不同来源（如模型参数不确定性）对投影方差的贡献。

Result: BMA估算的TCRE的90%置信区间与IPCC AR6高度重叠，但均值更高、标准差更小；2100年全球升温预测结果略高于现有方法且不确定性更低；样本外验证显示方法校准良好；方差分解表明模型参数不确定性是投影方差的主要来源。

Conclusion: 基于BMA的全统计方法能客观估算TCRE，减少主观偏差，并清晰划分不确定性来源，为气候政策提供更可靠、更透明的升温预估，同时便于后续验证与改进。

Abstract: The Transient Climate Response to cumulative CO2 Emissions (TCRE) is a key metric for linking greenhouse gas emissions to global temperature change and informing climate policy. However, extant estimates of the TCRE often depend on subjective model selection or assumed sensitivity ranges, with limited validation against observed data. We develop a fully statistical data-driven approach using a Bayesian Model Averaging (BMA) approach to estimate the TCRE. This uses 37 climate models from the Coupled Model Intercomparison Project phase 6 (CMIP6), weighted according to their consistency with observed temperature data. Compared to the Intergovernmental Panel on Climate Change (IPCC)'s Sixth Assessment Report (AR6) TCRE estimate, our BMA approach yields a very likely range (90% interval) that overlaps substantially with that from the AR6, but with a higher mean and a lower standard deviation. The resulting projections of global temperature change to 2100 show somewhat higher warming and less uncertainty than some current methods. The use of statistical modeling methods makes it easier to validate the approach and to partition the uncertainty according to its sources. Out-of-sample predictive validation shows the method to be well calibrated. Variance decomposition shows model parameter uncertainty to be a main source of projection variance.

</details>


### [257] [Calibrated Bayesian Inference for Stochastic Intervention Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02924)
*Tyler M. Schmidt,Nathan B. Wikle*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种简单的后处理校正方法，可应用于非参数贝叶斯模型的后验样本，使得随机干预效应（stochastic intervention effects）的推断满足半参数Bernstein-von Mises定理，从而实现渐近有效的估计和具有频率学派覆盖率的可信区间，并通过新开发的SoftBART理论支持该方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果推断正从经典确定性处理效应（如ATE）扩展到随机干预效应，这类效应能弱化正定性要求且更具政策相关性。非参数贝叶斯模型虽具有灵活性和不确定性传播优势，但其后验不确定性在因果效应估计中未必得到良好校准（well calibrated）。因此需要一种不改变先验或拟合算法的校正方法，使贝叶斯推断在频率学派意义上具有有效覆盖。

Method: 作者提出一种后处理校正方法，直接对后验样本进行修正，无需更改先验或拟合算法。该方法适用于两类随机干预：（1）独立于观测处理过程的干预；（2）修改处理过程的干预，包括增量倾向得分干预（incremental propensity score interventions）和新提出的power-tilt干预。理论核心包括：证明校正后的后验满足半参数Bernstein-von Mises定理，从而实现渐近有效推断和可信区间的频率学派覆盖；并为SoftBART这一灵活的树基贝叶斯模型建立了新理论，给出了其支持随机干预效应校准贝叶斯推断的条件。

Result: 模拟研究表明，与未校正的贝叶斯分析相比，该校正方法能减少偏差并改善区间覆盖，同时与频率学派替代方法保持竞争力。实证应用中，该方法被用于估计在服用他汀类药物的几率发生假设性升高或降低时，预期LDL胆固醇水平的变化。

Conclusion: 该后处理校正方法在非参数贝叶斯框架下为随机干预效应提供了频率学派有效的推断，兼具贝叶斯灵活性与渐近校准性。SoftBART的新理论为该方法提供了坚实支撑，整体框架适用于多种随机干预类型，在理论和实践上均表现出色。

Abstract: Causal inference increasingly extends beyond classical causal effects defined by deterministic treatment assignments, such as the average treatment effect, to stochastic intervention effects that can weaken positivity requirements and offer greater policy relevance. Nonparametric Bayesian models are attractive for estimating these effects due to their flexibility and inherent uncertainty propagation, but this posterior uncertainty need not be well calibrated for the causal effect of interest. We develop a simple post-processing correction that can be applied to posterior samples without changing the prior or fitting algorithm. We prove that, for a broad class of stochastic interventions, the corrected posterior yields asymptotically efficient inference and credible intervals with asymptotically valid frequentist coverage; formally, it satisfies a semiparametric Bernstein-von Mises theorem. The theory covers interventions specified independently of the observed treatment process, as well as interventions that modify it, including incremental propensity score interventions and a new power-tilt intervention. A central contribution is new theory for SoftBART, including conditions under which this flexible tree-based Bayesian model supports calibrated Bayesian inference for stochastic intervention effects. In simulations, the correction reduces bias and improves coverage relative to the uncorrected Bayesian analysis while remaining competitive with frequentist alternatives. We illustrate the method by estimating how expected LDL cholesterol would change under hypothetical increases or decreases in the odds of receiving statin therapy.

</details>


### [258] [Weighted k-Sample Kolmogorov-Smirnov, Cramer-von Mises, and Anderson-Darling Tests for Assessing Covariate Balance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02929)
*Ariel Linden*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文将两组的加权分布协变量平衡检验扩展到任意k≥2组的情形，统一了KS、AD和CvM三种检验统计量，采用置换推断程序，并提供多重校正的事后两两比较方法。模拟研究表明，三类检验在不同差异模式下各有优势，但多组综合检验的功效普遍低于两组情形，而事后两两比较因不受零分布膨胀影响，往往更为灵敏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有加权分布检验仅适用于两组比较，无法处理多组（k≥2）协变量平衡检验的需求。研究者需要一种统一的框架，将Kolmogorov-Smirnov、Anderson-Darling和Cramer-von Mises检验推广到多组场景，并解决多组比较中综合检验功效下降的问题。

Method: 基于已有的k样本泛化方法，将KS、AD和CvM三种统计量扩展至任意加权组数k≥2，采用统一的置换推断程序进行显著性检验，并在k=2时精确退化为原始两组形式。同时提出事后两两比较程序，结合四种多重比较校正方法，在全域检验拒绝后定位具体存在差异的组别。

Result: 在k=3的四场景模拟研究中，I类错误率保持在名义水平附近。三种检验的优势模式得以保留：KS对中心位置差异最敏感，AD对尾部差异最敏感，AD与CvM对弥散性差异表现相当。综合检验功效普遍低于匹配的两组场景，原因并非差异被稀释，而是增加组别会扩增零分布本身。事后两两比较的统计量不受此惩罚，在怀疑特定组存在不平衡时更为灵敏。

Conclusion: 所提出的多组加权分布检验框架有效且稳健，三类统计量各具优势，事后两两比较程序弥补了综合检验的功效损失。方法已在Stata命令kstest、adtest和cvmtest中实现。

Abstract: Weighted distributional tests for covariate balance are currently limited to two-group comparisons. We extend the Kolmogorov-Smirnov, Anderson-Darling, and Cramer-von Mises tests to an arbitrary number of weighted groups k >= 2, using existing k-sample generalizations and a shared permutation-inference procedure; each statistic reduces exactly to its two-group counterpart at k = 2. An accompanying post-hoc pairwise procedure with four multiple-comparison adjustments localizes which groups differ following an omnibus rejection. In a four-scenario simulation study at k = 3, Type I error remained close to nominal, and the comparative advantages established for two groups were preserved: Kolmogorov-Smirnov was most powerful against a centrally located discrepancy, Anderson-Darling against a tail-located discrepancy, and Anderson-Darling and Cramer-von Mises performed comparably against a diffuse discrepancy. Omnibus power was nonetheless uniformly lower than in the matching two-group setting - not because the underlying discrepancy is diluted, but because adding groups enlarges the null distribution itself, so the post-hoc procedure, whose pairwise statistics carry no such penalty, is often the more sensitive tool whenever a specific group's imbalance is suspected. The methods are implemented in the Stata commands kstest, adtest, and cvmtest.

</details>


### [259] [A New Approach to Goodness of Fit for Ergodic Markov Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03088)
*Vance Martin,Yoshihiko Nishiyama,John Stachurski,Yiran Xie*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的基于密度的拟合优度检验方法，适用于遍历马尔可夫过程。该方法无需指定备择假设，无需估计平滑参数，且对1/√n局部备择具有非平凡的功效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的拟合优度检验方法在应用于遍历马尔可夫过程时，往往需要指定备择假设或估计平滑参数，这在实际应用中（尤其是计量经济学和金融建模）带来了不便。作者旨在提出一种无需这些要求、同时保持良好统计功效的新检验方法。

Method: 提出了一种基于密度的拟合优度检验，其核心思想是：将数据与零假设中所设定的模型类进行比较，若该类中没有任何模型能产生与数据匹配的平稳密度，则拒绝零假设。该方法通过比较密度来构建检验统计量，但巧妙避免了平滑参数的估计问题，并能在不指定备择假设的情况下实施。

Result: 该检验方法被证明对1/√n局部备择假设具有非平凡的功效（即检验能够以较高的概率识别出以1/√n速率收敛于零假设的偏离），这表明其具有良好的灵敏度。同时，该方法为计量经济学和金融建模中的若干现有问题提供了新的分析视角。

Conclusion: 该检验方法为遍历马尔可夫过程的模型验证提供了一种灵活且强大的工具，尤其适用于计量经济学和金融领域的实证研究，因为它无需预先指定备择假设，也规避了平滑参数选择这一常见难题。

Abstract: We introduce a new density-based goodness of fit test for ergodic Markov processes. Our test compares the data against the class of models specified in the null hypothesis, and rejects if no model in the class yields a stationary density that matches with the data. No alternative needs to be specified in order to implement the test. Although our test compares densities, estimation of smoothing parameters is not required, and the test has nontrivial power against $1/\sqrt{n}$ local alternatives. The test provides new perspectives on some existing problems in econometric and financial modeling.

</details>


### [260] [Estimating the Conditional Forecast-Revision Scale in Sequential Models: Local-Smoothing Limits, Matched Models, and Cost--Accuracy Trade-offs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03163)
*Hui-Mean Foo,Yuan-chin Ivan Chang*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文研究条件预测修正尺度（conditional forecast-revision scale）I_t 的估计问题。该尺度衡量由观测 X_t 引发的预测更新的历史特定幅度，但因其由两个未知条件均值构建的条件二阶矩，无法直接观测。作者比较了多种估计方法（块自助法、条件方差模型、状态空间模型、流式平滑器、已训练循环网络的遗忘门），通过误差分解区分一步预测误差与条件二阶矩追踪误差，揭示了不同结构假设下估计量的渐近性质与计算成本权衡，最终给出实用选择规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 条件预测修正尺度 I_t 在时间序列分析中具有重要理论意义，但因其依赖于未知条件均值而不可直接观测。现有文献缺乏对不同估计方法在结构假设、渐近性质与计算成本方面的系统比较，因此需要建立一套指导原则来选择合适且经济的估计量。

Method: 作者构建了误差分解框架，将总误差分为一步预测误差和条件二阶矩追踪误差两部分。在多种数据生成过程设计（波动率驱动与状态驱动）下，系统比较六种估计方法：块自助法（block bootstrap）、条件方差模型、拟合的状态空间模型、两种 O(1) 流式平滑器，以及已训练循环网络的遗忘门。通过理论分析与模拟实验评估各方法的渐近一致性、均方误差速率和计算成本。

Result: 外部调优的纯滞后平滑器在 I_t 于采样尺度变化时可能不一致，但在缓慢变化下达到 T^{-2/3} 的 MSE 速率（I_t 为 T^{-1/3}）；正确设定的状态空间估计量利用当前状态可突破此限制。波动率驱动设计中，条件方差模型作为点估计比块自助法准确且便宜百倍以上，后者的价值在于提供抽样分布。状态驱动设计中，仅结构匹配的滤波器能恢复快速变化。循环网络遗忘门无法直接追踪 I_t，但对其门向量进行监督线性探测则可，表明 I_t 是线性可解码而非免费可得。

Conclusion: 实用规则：识别条件二阶矩的结构特征，将估计量与之匹配，然后选择成本最低的胜任方法。不同结构假设下不存在统一最优估计量，选择应基于对数据生成过程的认知与计算预算约束。

Abstract: The \emph{conditional forecast-revision scale}
  $\It=\{\Var(\E[X_{t+1}\mid\F_t]\mid\F_{t-1})\}^{1/2}$ measures the
  history-specific size of the forecast update induced by observing $X_t$.
  Because it is a conditional second moment built from two unknown conditional
  means, it is not directly observed. We study which estimator of $\It$ should
  be used under different structural assumptions and computational budgets. The
  comparison includes a block bootstrap, a conditional-variance model, a fitted
  state-space model, two $O(1)$ streaming smoothers, and the forget gate of an
  already-trained recurrent network. An error decomposition separates
  one-step-prediction error from conditional-second-moment tracking error. We
  show that externally tuned lag-only smoothers can be inconsistent when $\It$
  changes at the sampling scale, although they attain the usual $T^{-2/3}$
  mean-squared-error rate ($T^{-1/3}$ for $\It$) under slow variation; a correctly
  specified state-space estimator escapes
  this limit by using the current state. In volatility-driven designs, a cheap
  conditional-variance model is more accurate and over one hundred times cheaper
  \emph{as a point estimator} than the implemented block bootstrap, whose value
  lies in the sampling distribution it provides rather than in point tracking. In state-driven designs,
  only the structurally matched filter recovers the fast variation. Read directly, a
  trained network's forget gate does not track $\It$ --- though a supervised linear
  probe on the full gate vector does, so $\It$ is linearly decodable but not
  available for free. These results yield a practical rule: identify
  the conditional-second-moment structure, match the estimator to it, and then
  choose the least costly adequate method.

</details>


### [261] [A Bures-Wasserstein Formulation of Matrix Decomposition Structural Equation Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03183)
*Naoto Yamashita*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文在矩阵分解SEM（MDSEM）与协方差结构方程模型之间建立了理论桥梁：通过单一损失函数重构MDSEM，证明其等价于最小化观测与模型隐含协方差矩阵之间的平方Bures-Wasserstein距离，从而将MDSEM纳入最小差异估计框架，推导出一致性、渐近正态性和标准误，并通过模拟验证其有限样本表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MDSEM作为一种基于数据矩阵而非协方差矩阵的结构方程建模替代方法，其与协方差SEM的理论关系及估计量的统计性质一直不明确，缺乏严格的理论基础。

Method: 1. 使用整合测量模型与结构模型的单一损失函数重新表述MDSEM；2. 证明该损失函数最小化等价于最小化观测与模型隐含协方差矩阵之间的平方Bures-Wasserstein距离；3. 基于该等价性，在最小差异估计框架下推导估计量的一致性、渐近正态性和标准误；4. 通过模拟研究评估有限样本表现、置信区间覆盖率和数值稳定性。

Result: 1. MDSEM估计量可同时视为MDSEM估计量和最小差异估计框架下的协方差SEM估计量；2. 有限样本表现与最大似然等传统估计量相当；3. 置信区间覆盖率接近名义水平；4. 在小样本和模型误设条件下比传统SEM估计量具有更好的数值稳定性。

Conclusion: Bures-Wasserstein公式为MDSEM提供了严格的理论基础，同时也为协方差SEM提供了一个具有实用价值的差异函数，兼具理论严谨性和数值稳定性优势。

Abstract: Matrix decomposition SEM (MDSEM) is a data-matrix-based alternative to conventional covariance-based SEM, but its theoretical relationship to covariance-based SEM and the statistical properties of its estimator have remained unclear. We first reformulate MDSEM using a single loss function that integrates the measurement and structural models. We then prove that minimizing this loss over the model parameters is equivalent to minimizing the squared Bures-Wasserstein (BW) distance between the observed and model-implied covariance matrices, and use this equivalence to establish the estimator's theoretical properties. Specifically, the equivalence identifies the proposed estimator as both an MDSEM estimator and a covariance-based SEM estimator within the minimum discrepancy estimation framework, from which consistency, asymptotic normality, and standard errors are derived. Simulations show that its finite-sample performance is comparable to that of conventional estimators, including maximum likelihood, and that confidence-interval coverage is close to the nominal level. The estimator is also more numerically stable than conventional SEM estimators in small samples and under model misspecification. Thus, the BW formulation provides a theoretical foundation for MDSEM and a practically useful discrepancy function for covariance-based SEM.

</details>


### [262] [Generalized propensity score weighting for functional causal inference framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03200)
*Simone Ciardulli,Nicole Fontana,Simone Vantini,Francesca Ieva*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种函数型倾向得分加权框架，用于在观测性研究中估计连续域上函数型处理的因果效应，通过消除时变处理与混杂因素间的依赖实现协变量平衡，支持函数型处理、协变量和结局的设置，并应用于UK Biobank数据估计BMI轨迹对2型糖尿病及糖化血红蛋白轨迹的因果效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统因果推断中倾向得分方法主要针对标量处理变量，当处理变量是定义在连续域上的函数（如随时间变化的轨迹）时，混杂调整变得极具挑战性。现有方法在计算效率、协变量平衡和估计精度方面存在不足，亟需一种能同时处理函数型处理、协变量和结局的通用框架。

Method: 提出函数型倾向得分加权框架，核心创新包括：(1) 通过消除时变处理与观测混杂因素之间的依赖来实现协变量平衡；(2) 提出权重估计问题的对偶形式，转化为光滑无约束优化问题以提升计算可扩展性；(3) 将框架自然扩展至时变协变量和纵向结局，通过函数对函数的边际结构模型（function-on-function marginal structural model）估计因果效应曲面。

Result: 与现有方法相比，所提方法在协变量平衡性、估计精度和计算效率方面均有显著提升，且这些优势在扩展至函数型协变量和结局时依然保持。在UK Biobank应用中，成功估计了BMI轨迹对2型糖尿病风险及后续糖化血红蛋白轨迹（代谢状态的功能性指标）的因果效应。

Conclusion: 该函数型倾向得分加权框架为函数型处理变量的因果推断提供了有效且可扩展的解决方案，兼顾统计性能与计算效率，能自然地推广至复杂的函数型数据场景，具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: Estimating causal effects in observational studies requires adjustment for confounding, a task that becomes challenging when the exposure is a function observed over a continuous domain rather than a scalar variable. We develop a functional propensity score weighting framework that achieves covariate balance by removing dependence between time-varying treatments and observed confounders, thereby enabling estimation of marginal causal effects in settings with functional treatments, covariates, and outcomes. We propose a dual formulation of the weight estimation problem that yields a smooth unconstrained optimization and improves computational scalability. The proposed framework extends naturally to settings with time-varying covariates and to longitudinal outcomes via a function-on-function marginal structural model, allowing estimation of causal effect surfaces. The proposed method improves covariate balance, estimation accuracy, and computational efficiency compared to the existing approach and retains these properties when extended to functional covariates and outcomes. We apply the method to data from the UK Biobank to estimate the causal effect of body mass index trajectories on the risk of Type 2 Diabetes and on subsequent glycated hemoglobin trajectories, a functional measure of metabolic status.

</details>


### [263] [Evaluating Treatment Effects using Group Testing with Retesting of Positive Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03224)
*Aye Aye Maung,Qi Zheng*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对群组检验设计的因果推断框架，将逆概率加权整合进合并伪似然中，以在存在基线混杂时无偏估计平均处理效应，并通过模拟和CDC流感疫苗有效性数据验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统群组检验方法仅限于关联分析，无法在个体层面存在基线混杂时推断干预的直接因果效应。该研究旨在填补群组检验设计与因果推断之间的这一方法论空白。

Method: 将逆概率加权原则直接整合进合并伪似然公式，构造无偏伪得分函数；基于该函数提出插件估计量，并在标准正则条件下证明其一致性与渐近正态性。

Result: 数值模拟表明，该方法能成功消除严重的选择偏倚，准确恢复真实平均处理效应，而传统未加权合并模型则失效。在CDC美国流感疫苗有效性网络的真实观测监测数据上，该方法展示了作为估计和诊断工具的实用性。

Conclusion: 所提出的因果群组检验框架有效桥接了群组检验与因果推断，能够在存在混杂时提供无偏的干预效应估计，具有重要的公共卫生应用前景。

Abstract: Group testing is an established, highly cost-effective strategy for population-level disease surveillance that identifies positive individuals by pooling biological specimens. Originally introduced during World War II for large-scale screening and heavily utilized in modern high-throughput public health infrastructure, traditional group testing methods are restricted to purely associational analyses. Consequently, they lack the capacity to infer the direct causal effect of an intervention when individual-level data are subject to baseline confounding. In this work, we bridge this fundamental gap by introducing a causal inference framework tailored specifically for group testing designs. We integrate the principles of inverse probability weighting (IPW) directly into a pooled pseudo-likelihood formulation to construct an unbiased pseudo-score function. Under standard regularity conditions, we prove the consistency and asymptotic normality of our proposed plug-in estimator. Extensive numerical simulations demonstrate that our framework successfully purges severe selection bias, accurately recovering the true average treatment effect where traditional unweighted pooling models fail. Finally, we illustrate the practical utility of our method as both an estimation and a diagnostic tool using real-world observational surveillance data from the CDC's U.S. Influenza Vaccine Effectiveness Network.

</details>


### [264] [Local Quasi-Linear Models: Kernel Differential Equation Regression and Fire Data Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03306)
*Chunlei Ge,W. John Braun*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了局部拟线性（LQL）模型，将微分方程约束下的局部多项式回归框架扩展至一般一阶线性常微分方程。推导了任意Taylor阶数的DE1-k估计量及其渐近偏倚与方差，给出了未知系数函数的两类估计方法。仿真表明自动阶数选择的DE1-k估计优于无约束局部线性回归，并在火灾火源燃烧速率实验中验证了其在稀疏数据场景下融合物理结构的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DE约束局部多项式回归仅限于指数增长模型（一阶线性ODE的特例），缺乏对一般形式 g'(x)=a(x)g(x)+b(x) 的统一框架；同时，在火灾科学等应用中，数据稀疏但物理机理已知，需要一种能有效融合机理约束与数据驱动的估计方法。

Method: 提出局部拟线性（LQL）模型，构建DE约束下的局部多项式估计量（DE1-k）。核心方法：(1) 通过约束局部多项式拟合，将ODE结构嵌入核加权最小二乘，推导任意Taylor阶数k的闭式估计量；(2) 建立渐近条件偏倚与方差的解析表达式；(3) 当a(x)和b(x)未知时，提出两类估计策略；(4) 采用自动阶数选择方法平衡估计误差与ODE一致性误差。

Result: 仿真研究在两个结构不同的ODE上显示，DE1-k + 自动阶数选择的估计误差和ODE一致性误差均低于无约束局部线性回归。在Albini(1979)火源燃烧速率实验中，DE约束估计量在最稀疏的树种-直径组中显著优于局部线性回归，物理结构有效补偿了数据不足。对模型误指定的稳健性分析表明，LQL模型在局部拟指数替代模型下仍保持合理表现。

Conclusion: LQL框架将DE约束回归范式从指数增长模型成功推广至一般一阶线性ODE，为火灾科学及其他具有机理知识但数据稀疏的应用领域提供了实用估计工具。该方法在物理建模与数据驱动之间实现了有效平衡。

Abstract: We introduce the local quasi-linear (LQL) model, a differential equation-constrained local polynomial regression framework for the general first-order linear ordinary differential equation (ODE) $g'(x)=a(x)g(x)+b(x)$, extending prior work on differential equation-constrained local polynomial regression (DE-constrained LPR) for the exponential growth model. We derive closed-form DE-constrained local polynomial (DE1-$k$) estimators for arbitrary Taylor degree $k$, establish their asymptotic conditional bias and variance, and propose two approaches for estimating $a(x)$ and $b(x)$ when they are unknown. A simulation study across two structurally different ODEs shows that DE1-$k$ estimation with automatic degree selection reduces both estimation error and ODE-consistency error relative to unconstrained local linear regression. We then apply the framework to the firebrand burning-rate experiment of Albini (1979), modelling the density-loss curve of wind-driven firebrands with a physically motivated forced-convection ODE; the DE-constrained estimator outperforms local linear regression in the sparsest species-diameter groups, where physical structure is most valuable in compensating for scarce data. We further examine the robustness of the LQL model to misspecification against a local quasi-exponential alternative. Together, these results extend the DE-constrained regression paradigm to a broad class of physically motivated linear models and provide a practical estimation tool for fire science and other application areas where mechanistic knowledge is available but data are sparse.

</details>


### [265] [Matrix Decomposition Latent Growth Model Tree](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03377)
*Naoya Todo,Naoto Yamashita,Satoshi Usami*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了矩阵分解潜变量增长模型树（MDLGM Tree），将基于特征值分解的矩阵分解因子分析（MDFA）方法引入SEM Tree框架，以解决传统SEM Tree在纵向数据分类中计算成本高和不当解频发的问题。模拟研究表明，MDLGM Tree大幅减少了不当解的发生，且计算效率显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统SEM Tree以潜变量增长模型（LGM）为模板模型虽可用于纵向数据分类，但面临两大实际障碍：一是计算成本高，二是因模型误设或数据特征（如测量时点有限）导致的不当解频发。现有方法未能有效解决这些问题。

Method: 作者开发了MDLGM Tree方法，将矩阵分解因子分析（MDFA）——一种基于特征值分解的模型估计方法——应用于LGM的参数估计，并嵌入SEM Tree的递归分区框架中。该方法替代了传统最大似然估计，通过在模拟研究中系统比较MDLGM Tree与传统SEM Tree在不当解发生率和计算时间上的表现来验证其有效性。

Result: 模拟研究结果表明，与传统SEM Tree相比，MDLGM Tree显著减少了不当解的发生，同时在计算时间方面表现出极高的效率。

Conclusion: MDLGM Tree是一种有效且高效的纵向数据分类方法，通过MDFA估计替代最大似然估计，成功克服了传统SEM Tree的不当解问题和计算成本瓶颈，具有良好的应用前景。

Abstract: Latent growth models (LGMs) have been widely used to describe individual trajectories of change, average change patterns, and individual differences in change within a group. Structural equation model tree (SEM Tree) partitions samples through recursive partitioning using external covariates and characterizes between-group differences via parameters of prespecified SEM template models. An SEM Tree with an LGM as its template model may therefore serve as a useful classification method for longitudinal data. However, current SEM Tree implementations may face important practical obstacles, including high computational cost and frequent improper solutions caused by model misspecification or data characteristics (e.g., a limited number of measurement occasions). In the present study, we developed a matrix decomposition LGM tree (MDLGM Tree), which applies the matrix decomposition factor analysis (MDFA) approach, an eigenvalue-decomposition-based method for model estimation. Previous studies on MDFA and its extensions to SEM have shown that this approach can reduce the risk of improper solutions while yielding estimates highly similar to those obtained by maximum likelihood estimation. Through a series of simulations, we demonstrated that, compared with conventional SEM Tree, the MDLGM Tree greatly reduces the occurrence of improper solutions and is also highly efficient in terms of computation time.

</details>


### [266] [Multidimensional Integral Fractional Ornstein--Uhlenbeck Process with an Application to Animal Movement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03381)
*J. H. Ramírez-González,Erick A. Chacón-Montalván,Paula Moraga,Ying Sun*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文将积分分数Ornstein-Uhlenbeck (ifOU) 过程推广到多维情形，用于动物遥测建模。三个坐标轴的速度分量由多元分数布朗运动驱动的独立fOU过程表示，各坐标拥有各自的阻尼、尺度和Hurst参数。论文建立了协方差有效性、交叉相关容许区域及渐近性质，并开发了仿真、似然推断和速度重建方法，最终应用于德国迁徙中的夜蝠遥测数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动物遥测数据（经度、纬度、海拔速度）需要能刻画时间依赖性和长程记忆的连续时间模型。现有ifOU过程仅限于单维，无法同时处理三维轨迹中各坐标间的交叉相关及各自不同的记忆特性，因此需要将其推广到多维设定。

Method: 将ifOU过程扩展为多维形式：每个坐标的速度由独立fOU过程建模，驱动项为多元分数布朗运动（mfBm）。各坐标保留独立的阻尼参数、尺度参数和Hurst指数。理论上推导了交叉协方差的有效性条件、容许的交叉相关区域以及交叉协方差与分离增量的渐近行为。方法上开发了有限维仿真算法、高斯似然推断程序和条件速度重建技术。

Result: 通过重复仿真实验考察了交叉坐标相关性的估计性能；在一个三维轨迹上展示了完整参数向量的联合估计。将该模型应用于德国五只普通夜蝠（Nyctalus noctula）迁徙的遥测记录，其中三条轨迹包含海拔测量数据。

Conclusion: 多维ifOU过程能够灵活捕捉动物遥测中三维速度的长程依赖性及各坐标的不同记忆特性，同时保持经典OU过程的特例。该方法在理论和计算上均具可行性，适用于真实生态数据的建模与分析。

Abstract: Fractional Ornstein--Uhlenbeck (fOU) processes model temporal dependence and memory, including long-range dependence, while retaining the classical Ornstein--Uhlenbeck process as a special case. We extend the integral fractional Ornstein--Uhlenbeck (ifOU) process to a multidimensional setting for animal telemetry. Longitude, Latitude, and Altitude velocities are represented by coordinate-specific fOU processes driven by a multivariate fractional Brownian motion, allowing each coordinate to retain its own damping, scale, and Hurst parameters. We establish covariance validity, characterize the admissible cross-correlation region, and derive the asymptotic behavior of cross-covariances and separated increments. We develop procedures for finite-dimensional simulation, Gaussian likelihood inference, and conditional velocity reconstruction. Replicated simulations examine estimation of cross-coordinate correlations, while joint estimation of the complete parameter vector is illustrated for one three-dimensional trajectory. The proposed model is applied to telemetry records from five common noctule bats migrating in Germany, including three trajectories with Altitude measurements.

</details>


### [267] [Confidence Horizons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03889)
*Chase Mathis,Ian Waudby-Smith*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了"置信水平线"（confidence horizons），一种在有限时间范围内实现更精确大样本随时有效推断的统计工具，并给出了其渐近分位数的闭式分布函数，避免了传统群序贯方法中的重复积分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的随时有效推断方法为保证在无限时间范围内的有效性而趋于保守；实践中因预算、操作或伦理约束，时间范围往往有限。本文探讨是否可通过放弃有限范围之外的有效性来换取更精确的推断。

Method: 提出"置信水平线"族：可视为有限时间范围上的大样本置信序列，或具有最大期中窥视次数的群序贯重复置信区间。建立了与Pocock、O'Brien-Fleming、Wang-Tsiatis群序贯边界的显式联系，并推导了相关统计量的闭式分布函数，用于精确计算渐近分位数。

Result: 给出了置信水平线渐近分位数的精确计算方法，绕过了传统群序贯方法的重复积分。在自适应Neyman分配的序贯随机实验中展示了置信水平线用于处理效应估计的效果。

Conclusion: 放弃无限时间范围的有效性可以显著提升有限范围内随时有效推断的精度；置信水平线为此提供了理论支撑和实用的计算工具。

Abstract: Anytime-valid inference enables analysts to continuously monitor their data and stop experiments early. However, the majority of these methods incur a certain conservativeness by remaining valid on infinite time horizons. In practice, a bound on the horizon may be imposed due to budgetary, practical, or ethical constraints. In this paper, we ask the question: "Is it possible to obtain sharper large-sample anytime-valid inference by forgoing validity beyond some finite time horizon?". We provide a positive answer to this question by proposing a family of statistical objects that we call "confidence horizons". These objects can be viewed as large-sample confidence sequences on bounded time horizons, or alternatively as group sequential repeated confidence intervals with a maximal number of interim peeking times. We make explicit connections to the group sequential boundaries of Pocock [1977], O'Brien--Fleming [1979], and Wang--Tsiatis [1987]. We derive closed-form distribution functions of certain statistics which can be used to calculate the asymptotic quantiles of confidence horizons exactly, sidestepping the repeated integration typically employed in group sequential methods. We illustrate the use of confidence horizons for treatment effect estimation in sequentially randomized experiments under adaptive Neyman allocation.

</details>


### [268] [Reusing Operational Evidence After Context Changes: A Conservative Bayesian Framework for Autonomous Vehicle Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03384)
*Robab Aghazadeh Chakherlou,Siddartha Khastgir,Xingyu Zhao*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文研究了当运行环境发生变化时，如何利用保守贝叶斯推理（CBI）将先前目标运行域（TOD）中的运行证据保守地迁移至新的TOD，以支持变更后的可靠性声明，避免全量复用或全部丢弃的极端做法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运行证据的收集成本高昂，当系统部署的运行环境发生变化时，旧环境中积累的无故障运行证据在新环境中的相关性变得不确定。现有实践中，要么完全复用、要么完全丢弃的做法均不理想——尤其当有理由相信新环境不比旧环境更差但该信念本身存在不确定性时。因此需要一种能折中处理、量化不确定性的证据迁移方法。

Method: 采用保守贝叶斯推理（CBI）方法：通过约束一组可容许的先验分布，将新TOD中的证据与来自先前TOD的加权证据及部分先验知识相结合。CBI不对先验做单一假定，而是在先验约束下产生感兴趣量的保守后验界。

Result: 通过数值算例展示了：来自先前TOD的既有运行证据可以被保守且透明地迁移，用于支持变更后TOD中的可靠性声明。

Conclusion: CBI提供了一种在运行环境变更后，以保守、透明的方式复用历史运行证据的可行方案，避免了全有或全无的证据处理策略，为变更后的系统安全/可靠性论证提供了更合理的支撑。

Abstract: Operational evidence, i.e., evidence of operation without failure is an important component of confidence in the safety or reliability of a system in service, but it is costly to collect. When the context of operation changes, the relevance of previously collected operational evidence becomes unclear. This problem arises, for example, when an autonomous vehicle that has operated safely in one operational environment (the Target Operational Domain, TOD) is deployed in a different but related TOD. Existing practice often treats such evidence in an all-or-nothing manner: either it is fully reused, or it is discarded. Neither position is satisfactory when there are good reasons to believe that the new context is no worse than the previous one, but that belief is itself uncertain. This paper studies how to make post-change reliability claims by combining evidence from two TODs using Conservative Bayesian Inference (CBI), which combines the evidence from the new context with a weighted amount of previous context and partial prior knowledge through constraints on a set of admissible priors. This yields conservative posterior bounds on quantities of interest. A numerical example illustrates how pre-existing evidence from a previous TOD can be transferred, conservatively and transparently, to support reliability claims in the changed TOD.

</details>


### [269] [Fast covariance-free spatiotemporal modeling via coarse-to-fine learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03449)
*Daisuke Murakami*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: CF-STM 是一种协方差无关的粗到细时空建模框架，通过多尺度局部加权模型解耦空间表示与时序推断，在保持预测精度的同时大幅降低计算成本，并已实现为 R 包 spCF。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统可扩展时空建模方法依赖协方差模型，将空间表示与时序推断紧密耦合，导致计算成本高昂。需要一种能解耦空间与时间建模、灵活指定时序模型且计算可扩展的新框架。

Method: 提出 CF-STM 框架，将粗到细空间建模（CF-SM）扩展至时空场景：利用多尺度局部加权模型表示潜在空间过程，在局部中心点通过状态空间模型单独建模时序依赖，实现无协方差矩阵的协方差无关形式。

Result: 蒙特卡洛实验表明，CF-STM 在预测性能上与现有可扩展时空模型相当，但计算成本显著更低；在东京都市圈长期住宅用地价格数据应用中，CF-STM 能灵活捕捉复杂时空模式并提供可解释的推断。

Conclusion: CF-STM 成功实现了空间表示与时序建模的解耦，兼顾了预测精度、计算效率和可解释性，为大规模时空数据分析提供了实用工具，并以 R 包 spCF 形式开源供社区使用。

Abstract: Scalable spatiotemporal modeling remains challenging because conventional methods rely on covariance models that tightly couple spatial representation and temporal inference, often leading to high computational costs. To address this difficulty, we developed coarse-to-fine spatiotemporal modeling (CF-STM), a framework that extends coarse-to-fine spatial modeling (CF-SM) to spatiotemporal settings. CF-STM represents latent spatial processes through multiscale locally weighted models, with temporal dependence modeled separately through state-space models defined at local centers. This covariance-free formulation achieves scalable computation while allowing temporal models to be flexibly specified without altering the spatial representation. Monte Carlo experiments demonstrate predictive performance comparable to that of alternative scalable space-time models at a substantially lower computational cost.
