An application to long-term residential land price data in the Tokyo metropolitan area shows that CF-STM flexibly captures complex spatiotemporal patterns while enabling interpretable inferences. CF-STM is implemented in an R package spCF (https://cran.r-project.org/web/packages/spCF/).

</details>


### [270] [Two-Stage Design with Sample Size Re-estimation Using gsDesign](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03456)
*Keaven M. Anderson,Alison Pedley*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文比较了组序贯设计与基于条件把握度的样本量重估设计，指出尽管条件把握度设计在某些情况下可能有微小优势，但很容易产生效率极低的设计，且可能泄露中期治疗效果信息，因此组序贯设计通常仍是更优选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床试验开始前，效应量和冗余参数往往不充分了解。保守规划的组序贯设计虽能通过提前停止规则调整样本量，但存在中期分析后仍有大量患者入组的"入组超跑"问题。条件把握度设计被提出用于缓解该问题，并声称可减少前期计划样本量。本文旨在系统比较两类设计的效率与优劣。

Method: 利用R包gsDesign推导2阶段组序贯设计和条件把握度设计；通过期望样本量计算，在可比检验效能下对两种设计进行比较分析。

Result: 条件把握度设计在某些场景下可能具有微小优势，但极易被推导出效率极低的方案；此外，条件把握度设计可能在一定程度上暴露中期治疗效应信息。

Conclusion: 组序贯设计在大多数情况下仍是临床试验设计的首选。条件把握度设计应谨慎使用，因其效率不稳定且存在信息泄露风险。

Abstract: The effect size and nuisance parameters needed to appropriately size a clinical trial are generally not adequately understood before a trial begins. Through pre-planned early stopping rules, a conservatively planned group sequential design can effectively adapt the sample size for a clinical trial for a new treatment that is ineffective, very effective or minimally effective. One potential issue with such designs is that a substantial number of patients can be enrolled after a data cutoff for an interim analysis while data are being entered, cleaned, analyzed and discussed. A strategy of re-estimating the sample size at an interim analysis based on conditional power has been proposed to reduce somewhat this enrollment overrun issue. A purported advantage sometimes claimed is a smaller up-front planned sample size than a conservatively planned group sequential design. We demonstrate derivation of 2-stage group sequential and conditional power designs using the gsDesign R package and suggest comparing designs with comparable power using expected sample size calculations. While there are cases where conditional power designs may have small advantages, it is quite easy to derive very inefficient conditional power designs. This, along with the fact that a conditional power design may reveal something about the interim treatment effect, will often leave a group sequential design as the design of choice.

</details>


### [271] [Beyond Predicting Responses: Conformal Inference for Latent Distributional Parameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03607)
*Minxing Zheng,Wenbin Zhou,Shixiang Zhu*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出了LatentCP——一种无需先验的共形推断框架，用于在无法观测隐参数的情况下，仅基于观测到的上下文-响应对和已知的前向模型，构建隐参数的不确定性集合。通过响应空间的共形预测集反演至隐空间，并引入多层级聚合机制提升效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多预测问题需要推断控制响应分布的未观测实例特定隐参数，但隐参数在历史和未来实例中均不可得。现有方法（经验贝叶斯、似然方法、代理标签共形方法）在弱识别、观测不可辨识性、隐参数异质性和多模态等场景下覆盖率严重不足。需要一种不依赖隐参数校准标签、唯一逆映射或隐混合分布知识的方法。

Method: 提出LatentCP框架：(1) 在可观测响应空间构建共形预测集；(2) 根据候选隐参数所诱导的响应分布赋予该预测集的概率，决定是否保留该隐参数，实现从响应空间到隐空间的反演。该方法提供有限样本边缘覆盖保证。此外，针对隐集效率对响应空间误覆盖率的非单调依赖问题，提出多层级聚合过程：在多个响应集上聚合归一化不兼容得分，并利用独立调优样本选择聚合分布。

Result: 在合成实验中，LatentCP在弱前向识别、观测不可辨识性、隐异质性和多模态等条件下均保持标称的隐空间覆盖率，而经验贝叶斯、似然方法和代理标签共形方法出现严重覆盖不足。在加州野火真实数据集上，生成了空间自适应的隐火强度不确定性集。独立调优提升了效率，多层级聚合在不同响应水平包含互补信息时提供了额外增益，且不牺牲有效性。

Conclusion: LatentCP是一种有效的无先验共形框架，能够在弱识别和不可辨识等挑战性条件下为隐分布参数提供有限样本覆盖保证。多层级聚合和独立调优策略在不损害有效性的前提下显著提升效率，具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: Many prediction problems seek to infer an unobserved, instance-specific parameter that governs the distribution of an observable response, even though the latent parameter is unavailable for both historical and future instances. We develop LatentCP, a prior-free conformal framework that constructs uncertainty sets for latent distributional parameters using only observed context--response pairs and a specified forward model. The method first constructs a conformal prediction set in the observable response space and then retains candidate latent parameters according to the probability their induced response distributions assign to that set. This inversion provides finite-sample marginal coverage without requiring latent calibration labels, a unique inverse mapping, or knowledge of the latent mixing distribution. Because latent-set efficiency depends nonmonotonically on the response-space miscoverage rate, we further introduce a multilevel procedure that aggregates normalized incompatibility scores across several response sets and selects the aggregation distribution using an independent tuning sample. Across synthetic experiments, LatentCP maintains nominal latent coverage under weak forward identification, observational nonidentifiability, and latent heterogeneity and multimodality, where empirical-Bayes, likelihood-based, and proxy-label conformal methods can substantially under-cover. On a California wildfire real dataset, it produces spatially adaptive uncertainty sets for latent fire intensity. Independent tuning improves efficiency, while multilevel aggregation provides additional gains when different response levels contain complementary information, without sacrificing validity.

</details>


### [272] [A New Look at Gaussian Mixtures in the Presence of Missing-at-Random Responses and Covariates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03757)
*Hung Tong,Antonio Punzo,Cristina Tortora*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在MAR缺失机制下，对多响应-多协变量多元线性回归模型进行最大似然估计的方法。通过条件-边缘分解的多元高斯联合分布建模，利用EM算法进行参数估计与缺失值填补，并进一步拓展至基于模型的聚类框架（混合多元线性回归），实现了不完整数据下的软聚类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缺失值是统计建模中的常见挑战。在MAR机制下（缺失概率仅依赖已观测数据），现有方法在处理响应变量和协变量同时存在缺失的多元回归建模方面仍有不足，需要一种能同时处理响应与协变量缺失、并保持两者区分角色的统一框架。

Method: 采用条件-边缘分解对多元高斯联合分布进行重参数化，将变量自然划分为响应和协变量两部分；在最大似然框架下使用EM算法进行参数估计，同时实现缺失值的填补；进一步将该框架推广至混合多元线性回归模型，实现不完整数据下的软聚类。

Result: 模拟研究验证了方法的有效性；在包含缺失值的Automobile数据集上，所提出的重参数化方法展现出优势。该方法被认为是当前文献中处理回归数据聚类最通用的基于模型的解决方案之一。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法能够在MAR缺失机制下有效处理响应和协变量同时缺失的多元线性回归估计与聚类问题，通过条件-边缘分解的重参数化保留了响应与协变量的不同角色，具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: Missing values present a common challenge in statistical modeling, so handling them properly is an important research direction. Among the various mechanisms that can generate missing values, the most common is the missing-at-random (MAR) mechanism, in which the probability of missingness depends only on observed data and not on unobserved data.
  This paper addresses the problem of estimating a multivariate linear regression model with multiple random covariates in the presence of MAR values in both the response and covariate spaces using a maximum likelihood (ML) framework.
  The proposed methodology models the joint distribution of responses and covariates through a conditional-marginal factorization of a multivariate Gaussian distribution. This formulation can be interpreted as a reparameterization of the multivariate normal distribution when the variables can be naturally partitioned into responses and covariates.
  Parameter estimation is performed using the expectation-maximization (EM) algorithm, which facilitates the imputation of missing values while preserving the distinct roles of responses and covariates.
  We extend this framework to the model-based clustering setting by considering a mixture of multivariate linear regressions with multiple random covariates. This extension enables soft clustering under incomplete data and accommodates MAR values in both the multivariate responses and covariates. Hence, it represents one of the most general model-based clustering solutions for regression data currently available in the literature.
  The effectiveness of the methodology is demonstrated through a simulation study, and the advantages of the proposed reparameterization are illustrated using the Automobile dataset, which contains missing values.

</details>


### [273] [Multi-Signal Safety Surveillance with Bayesian Latent Factor Modeling and Bias Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03775)
*Ziyang Pan,Fan Bu*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一个多信号贝叶斯序贯监测框架，将基于阴性对照的实证偏倚校正与低秩潜在因子建模相结合，在重复监测多个暴露-结局信号时实现偏倚校正和信息共享。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全监测框架要么仅关注利用阴性对照校正残余偏倚，要么仅关注在暴露-结局对之间借用信息，缺乏同时兼顾两者的方法。在观测性医疗数据中，稀疏信息、相关信号间的依赖性以及系统性误差使得推断复杂化，亟需一个统一的解决方案。

Method: 提出多信号贝叶斯序贯监测框架，分两步：首先，利用假定潜在效应为零的阴性对照结局，通过层次贝叶斯模型在每个分析时间点学习暴露特异性的偏倚分布；其次，以这些分布为条件，在感兴趣的暴露和结局之间估计低秩潜在因子，实现跨相关信号的信息共享。随着新数据积累，后验推断序贯更新，输出经偏倚校正的多信号效应量后验摘要。

Result: 在一项使用美国大型保险理赔数据库的上市后疫苗安全监测研究中验证了该方法，展示了其在真实场景中的适用性。

Conclusion: 该框架成功整合了阴性对照偏倚校正与低秩潜在因子建模，能够在序贯监测中同时处理系统性误差和借用跨信号信息，为观测性医疗数据的大规模安全监测提供了有力的统计工具。

Abstract: Safety surveillance increasingly involves repeated monitoring of many exposure-outcome signals in observational healthcare data, where sparse information, dependence across related signals, and systematic error can complicate inference. Existing frameworks typically focus on either correcting residual bias using negative controls or borrowing information across exposure-outcome pairs, but not both. We propose a multi-signal Bayesian sequential surveillance framework that integrates empirical bias correction with low-rank latent factor modeling. At each analysis time, a hierarchical Bayesian model learns exposure-specific bias distributions from negative control outcomes assumed to have null latent effects. Conditional on these distributions, low-rank latent factors are estimated across exposures and outcomes of interest to share information across correlated signals. As new data accrue, posterior inference is updated sequentially, yielding bias-corrected posterior summaries of effect sizes across multiple monitored signals. We illustrate the method in a postmarket vaccine safety surveillance study using a large US insurance claims database.

</details>


### [274] [Reduced-rank Generalized Bilinear Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03832)
*Kevin S. Kapner,Jeffrey W. Miller*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出降秩广义双线性模型（RR-GBM），通过降秩样本系数矩阵在高维协变量场景下实现高效降维与效应估计，在模拟和胰腺癌Perturb-seq数据中验证了其统计与计算优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准广义双线性模型（GBM）在样本协变量数量增加时，统计推断精度和计算效率急剧下降，亟需一种能够处理大量协变量同时控制参数规模的方法。

Method: 引入降秩样本系数矩阵替代全秩矩阵，大幅减少参数数量；同时开发了基于数据稀疏化（data thinning）的模型选择方法以实现秩的选择，并提出新的协变量与特征关系可视化策略。

Result: 模拟研究表明，当真实系数矩阵为降秩或接近降秩时，RR-GBM在估计精度和计算速度上均优于全秩GBM；在胰腺癌Perturb-seq数据应用中展示了方法的实用性和可视化能力。

Conclusion: RR-GBM有效解决了GBM在高维协变量下的效率瓶颈，兼具统计准确性与计算可行性，为基因组学等高维数据分析提供了新的有力工具。

Abstract: Dimensionality reduction and effect estimation are central tasks in the analysis of high-dimensional data such as in genomics. Generalized bilinear models (GBMs) provide a versatile framework for these tasks, however, the statistical and computational efficiency of GBMs degrades rapidly as the number of sample covariates grows. To address this limitation, we introduce reduced-rank generalized bilinear models (RR-GBMs), which employ a reduced-rank sample coefficient matrix to model the effect of a large number of covariates without requiring an excessive number of parameters. In simulation studies, we find that when the true sample coefficient matrix is reduced rank or close to reduced rank, RR-GBM outperforms the standard full-rank GBM both statistically and computationally, providing more accurate estimates with lower computational burden. We develop a data thinning approach for model selection in the RR-GBM framework, facilitating rank selection. Furthermore, RR-GBM enables a new approach to visualizing the relationships among covariates and among features. We demonstrate the method in an application to Perturb-seq data for pancreatic cancer.

</details>


### [275] [Resolution-Adaptive Compact-Support Priors for Bayesian Wavelet Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03946)
*Nilotpal Sanyal*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出一种分辨率自适应的贝叶斯小波去噪方法，采用spike-and-slab先验，其slab部分为Wendland多项式核与半圆密度的混合，具有良好的理论与计算性质，在高斯误差仿真中表现优异，尤其低SNR下超越多种主流方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有小波去噪方法在分辨率层级间缺乏灵活的自适应收缩行为，需要一种能够在不同分辨率层级平滑调节稀疏性与收缩强度的先验结构，同时保持计算可行性和理论上的良好性质。

Method: 构建分辨率自适应的Bayesian小波去噪框架：(1) 提出spike-and-slab先验，slab为紧支撑Wendland型多项式核与半圆密度的混合；(2) Wendland成分在零附近集中质量并在边界平滑消失，半圆成分提供更分散的分布以适配不同分辨率层级；(3) 在平方误差损失下推导后验均值估计量，建立对称性、有界性、连续性及极限性质；(4) 在Laplace工作似然下Wendland贡献具有有限和解析表达式，半圆贡献通过稳定一维积分计算；(5) 开发经验贝叶斯超参数估计流程。

Result: 在Bumps、Blocks、Doppler、HeaviSine四个标准测试信号上的仿真显示：(1) 高斯似然版本在36个实验单元中的26个表现最强（非NLP方法中），包括所有低SNR单元；(2) 计算效率显著优于Laplace似然版本；(3) 在2008年Chino Hills地震加速度记录分析中，有效衰减快速波动同时保留主要加速度事件。

Conclusion: 所提分辨率自适应贝叶斯小波去噪方法通过Wendland-半圆混合slab先验，在不同分辨率层级实现灵活的自适应收缩，兼具理论严密性、计算可行性和优异的去噪性能，低SNR场景下优势尤为突出。

Abstract: We propose a resolution-adaptive Bayesian wavelet denoising method for noisy one-dimensional signals. The model uses a spike-and-slab prior whose continuous slab is a mixture of a compactly supported Wendland-type polynomial kernel and the semicircle density, with a data-adaptive resolution-specific truncation scale. The Wendland component concentrates mass near zero and vanishes smoothly at the support boundary, whereas the semicircle component is more dispersed, allowing the shrinkage rule to adapt its behavior across resolution levels. Under squared-error loss, we derive the posterior-mean estimator, establish key symmetry, boundedness, continuity, and limiting properties, define pointwise fixed-hyperparameter bias, variance, and risk, and develop an empirical-Bayes estimation procedure. The Wendland contribution has finite-sum expressions under a Laplace working likelihood, while the semicircle contribution is evaluated by stable one-dimensional integration. Simulations using the Bumps, Blocks, Doppler, and HeaviSine signals compare the proposed Gaussian- and Laplace-likelihood versions with universal thresholding, false-discovery-rate (FDR) thresholding, cross-validation (CV), Stein's unbiased risk estimate (SURE), the Bayesian adaptive multiresolution shrinker (BAMS), and a nonlocal-prior (NLP)-based method. In the primary Gaussian-error simulation study, the Gaussian-likelihood version was the strongest non-NLP method in 26 of the 36 design cells, including all low signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) cells, and had a substantially more favorable computational profile than the Laplace-likelihood version. Analysis of a seismic acceleration trace from the 2008 Chino Hills earthquake illustrates attenuation of rapid fluctuations and preservation of the dominant acceleration event under the chosen diagnostics.

</details>
